     1→# Database Improvements + Turso Integration Implementation Plan
     2→
     3→> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.
     4→
     5→**Goal:** Replace rusqlite with libsql (Turso-compatible), split db.rs into domain modules, add versioned migrations, improve resilience, harden event batching, move orchestrator state into SQLite, and add soft deletes.
     6→
     7→**Architecture:** Swap the sync rusqlite driver for async-native libsql, eliminating the Arc<Mutex> + spawn_blocking wrapper. Split the monolithic 1500+ line db.rs into domain-specific modules behind a single DbHandle. Use PRAGMA user_version for migration tracking. Support Turso embedded replicas when env vars are set, falling back to local SQLite.
     8→
     9→**Tech Stack:** Rust, libsql crate, dotenvy, tokio, axum

   218→
   219→### Task 3: Create db/migrations.rs — Versioned Migration Runner
   220→
   221→**Files:**
   222→- Create: `src/factory/db/migrations.rs`
   223→
   224→**Step 1: Write the migration runner**
   225→
   226→```rust
   227→use anyhow::{Context, Result};
   228→use libsql::Connection;
   229→
   230→const MIGRATIONS: &[(i64, &str)] = &[
   231→    (1, include_str!("migrations/001_initial.sql")),
   232→    (2, include_str!("migrations/002_github_integration.sql")),
   233→    (3, include_str!("migrations/003_agent_teams.sql")),
   234→    (4, include_str!("migrations/004_settings.sql")),
   235→    (5, include_str!("migrations/005_orchestrator_state.sql")),
   236→    (6, include_str!("migrations/006_soft_deletes_and_indexes.sql")),
   237→];
   238→
   239→/// Run all pending migrations. Uses PRAGMA user_version to track state.
   240→pub async fn run_migrations(conn: &Connection) -> Result<()> {
   241→    let current_version = get_user_version(conn).await?;
   242→
   243→    // Bootstrap: if tables exist but user_version is 0 (pre-migration DB),
   244→    // detect existing schema and set version accordingly.
   245→    if current_version == 0 {
   246→        let bootstrapped = bootstrap_existing_db(conn).await?;
   247→        if bootstrapped > 0 {
   248→            tracing::info!("Bootstrapped existing database at migration version {bootstrapped}");
   249→            return run_from_version(conn, bootstrapped).await;
   250→        }
   251→    }
   252→
   253→    run_from_version(conn, current_version).await
   254→}
   255→
   256→async fn run_from_version(conn: &Connection, current: i64) -> Result<()> {
   257→    for (version, sql) in MIGRATIONS.iter() {
   258→        if *version <= current {
   259→            continue;
   260→        }
   261→        tracing::info!("Running migration {version}...");
   262→        conn.execute_batch(sql)
   263→            .await
   264→            .with_context(|| format!("Failed to run migration {version}"))?;
   265→        set_user_version(conn, *version).await?;
   266→    }
   267→    Ok(())
   268→}
   269→
   270→async fn get_user_version(conn: &Connection) -> Result<i64> {
   271→    let mut rows = conn.query("PRAGMA user_version", ()).await
   272→        .context("Failed to query user_version")?;
   273→    match rows.next().await? {
   274→        Some(row) => Ok(row.get::<i64>(0)?),
   275→        None => Ok(0),
   276→    }
   277→}
   278→
   279→async fn set_user_version(conn: &Connection, version: i64) -> Result<()> {
   280→    conn.execute(&format!("PRAGMA user_version = {version}"), ())
   281→        .await
   282→        .with_context(|| format!("Failed to set user_version to {version}"))?;
   283→    Ok(())
   284→}
   285→
   286→/// Detect if this is a pre-migration database (tables exist but user_version=0).
   287→/// Returns the migration version that matches the current schema, or 0 if empty.
   288→async fn bootstrap_existing_db(conn: &Connection) -> Result<i64> {
   289→    // Check if the core 'projects' table exists
   290→    let mut rows = conn
   291→        .query(
   292→            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='projects'",
   293→            (),
   294→        )
   295→        .await?;
   296→
   297→    if rows.next().await?.is_none() {
   298→        return Ok(0); // Fresh database
   299→    }
   300→
   301→    // Tables exist — determine how far the schema has evolved.
   302→    // Check for settings table (migration 4)
   303→    let mut rows = conn
   304→        .query(
   305→            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='settings'",
   306→            (),
   307→        )
   308→        .await?;
   309→    if rows.next().await?.is_some() {
   310→        set_user_version(conn, 4).await?;
   311→        return Ok(4);
   312→    }
   313→
   314→    // Check for agent_teams table (migration 3)
   315→    let mut rows = conn
   316→        .query(
   317→            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='agent_teams'",
   318→            (),
   319→        )
   320→        .await?;
   321→    if rows.next().await?.is_some() {
   322→        set_user_version(conn, 3).await?;
   323→        return Ok(3);
   324→    }
   325→
   326→    // Check for github_repo column (migration 2)
   327→    let mut rows = conn
   328→        .query("PRAGMA table_info(projects)", ())
   329→        .await?;
   330→    while let Some(row) = rows.next().await? {
   331→        let name: String = row.get(1)?;
   332→        if name == "github_repo" {
   333→            set_user_version(conn, 2).await?;
   334→            return Ok(2);
   335→        }
   336→    }
   337→
   338→    // Base tables only (migration 1)
   339→    set_user_version(conn, 1).await?;
   340→    Ok(1)
   341→}
   342→

   344→mod tests {
   345→    use super::*;
   346→    use libsql::Builder;
   347→
   348→    async fn test_db() -> (libsql::Database, Connection) {
   349→        let db = Builder::new_local(":memory:").build().await.unwrap();
   350→        let conn = db.connect().unwrap();
   351→        (db, conn)
   352→    }
   353→
   354→    #[tokio::test]
   355→    async fn test_fresh_database_runs_all_migrations() {
   356→        let (_db, conn) = test_db().await;
   357→        run_migrations(&conn).await.unwrap();
   358→        let version = get_user_version(&conn).await.unwrap();
   359→        assert_eq!(version, 6);
   360→    }
   361→
   362→    #[tokio::test]
   363→    async fn test_idempotent_migration() {
   364→        let (_db, conn) = test_db().await;
   365→        run_migrations(&conn).await.unwrap();
   366→        // Running again should be a no-op
   367→        run_migrations(&conn).await.unwrap();
   368→        let version = get_user_version(&conn).await.unwrap();
   369→        assert_eq!(version, 6);
   370→    }
   371→
   372→    #[tokio::test]
   373→    async fn test_partial_migration_resumes() {
   374→        let (_db, conn) = test_db().await;
   375→        // Run only first 2 migrations manually
   376→        conn.execute_batch(MIGRATIONS[0].1).await.unwrap();
   377→        conn.execute_batch(MIGRATIONS[1].1).await.unwrap();
   378→        set_user_version(&conn, 2).await.unwrap();
   379→
   380→        // Now run_migrations should pick up from 3
   381→        run_migrations(&conn).await.unwrap();
   382→        let version = get_user_version(&conn).await.unwrap();
   383→        assert_eq!(version, 6);
   384→    }
   385→}

   400→
   401→### Task 4: Create db/mod.rs — DbHandle + Connection Factory
   402→
   403→This is the core rewrite. DbHandle changes from `Arc<Mutex<FactoryDb>>` to a thin wrapper around `libsql::Database`.
   404→
   405→**Files:**
   406→- Create: `src/factory/db/mod.rs`
   407→
   408→**Step 1: Write the new DbHandle**
   409→
   410→```rust
   411→pub mod agents;
   412→pub mod issues;
   413→pub mod migrations;
   414→pub mod pipeline;
   415→pub mod projects;
   416→pub mod settings;
   417→
   418→use std::path::Path;
   419→use std::time::Duration;
   420→
   421→use anyhow::{Context, Result};
   422→use libsql::{Builder, Connection, Database};
   423→
   424→/// Async database handle wrapping a libsql Database.
   425→///
   426→/// Supports two modes:
   427→/// - **Turso embedded replica** when TURSO_DATABASE_URL and TURSO_AUTH_TOKEN are set
   428→/// - **Local SQLite** otherwise (dev/offline fallback)
   429→#[derive(Clone)]
   430→pub struct DbHandle {
   431→    db: Database,
   432→}
   433→
   434→impl DbHandle {
   435→    /// Open a local-only SQLite database at the given path.
   436→    pub async fn new_local(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
   437→        let path_str = path.to_string_lossy().to_string();
   438→        let db = Builder::new_local(&path_str)
   439→            .build()
   440→            .await
   441→            .context("Failed to open local SQLite database")?;
   442→        let handle = Self { db };
   443→        handle.init().await?;
   444→        Ok(handle)
   445→    }
   446→
   447→    /// Open a Turso embedded replica with local file cache.
   448→    pub async fn new_remote_replica(
   449→        local_path: &Path,
   450→        url: &str,
   451→        token: &str,
   452→    ) -> Result<Self> {
   453→        let path_str = local_path.to_string_lossy().to_string();
   454→        let db = Builder::new_remote_replica(&path_str, url, token)
   455→            .sync_interval(Duration::from_secs(60))
   456→            .read_your_writes(true)
   457→            .build()
   458→            .await
   459→            .context("Failed to open Turso embedded replica")?;
   460→        let handle = Self { db };
   461→        handle.init().await?;
   462→        Ok(handle)
   463→    }
   464→
   465→    /// Open an in-memory database (for testing).
   466→    pub async fn new_in_memory() -> Result<Self> {
   467→        let db = Builder::new_local(":memory:")
   468→            .build()
   469→            .await
   470→            .context("Failed to open in-memory database")?;
   471→        let handle = Self { db };
   472→        handle.init().await?;
   473→        Ok(handle)
   474→    }
   475→
   476→    /// Get a new connection from the database.
   477→    pub fn conn(&self) -> Result<Connection> {
   478→        self.db.connect().context("Failed to get database connection")
   479→    }
   480→
   481→    /// Sync embedded replica with remote (no-op for local-only).
   482→    pub async fn sync(&self) -> Result<()> {
   483→        // sync() returns a result with replication info; we just need it to succeed
   484→        let _ = self.db.sync().await;
   485→        Ok(())
   486→    }
   487→
   488→    /// Initialize pragmas and run migrations.
   489→    async fn init(&self) -> Result<()> {
   490→        let conn = self.conn()?;
   491→        init_pragmas(&conn).await?;
   492→        migrations::run_migrations(&conn).await?;
   493→        Ok(())
   494→    }
   495→}
   496→
   497→/// Set WAL mode, busy timeout, and enable foreign keys.
   498→async fn init_pragmas(conn: &Connection) -> Result<()> {
   499→    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL", ())
   500→        .await
   501→        .context("Failed to set WAL mode")?;
   502→    conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000", ())
   503→        .await
   504→        .context("Failed to set busy_timeout")?;
   505→    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON", ())
   506→        .await
   507→        .context("Failed to enable foreign keys")?;
   508→    Ok(())
   509→}
   510→
   511→/// Run integrity check on startup. Logs warning but does not fail.
   512→pub async fn health_check(conn: &Connection) {
   513→    match conn.query("PRAGMA integrity_check", ()).await {
   514→        Ok(mut rows) => {
   515→            if let Ok(Some(row)) = rows.next().await {
   516→                if let Ok(result) = row.get::<String>(0) {
   517→                    if result != "ok" {
   518→                        tracing::warn!("Database integrity check failed: {result}");
   519→                    }
   520→                }
   521→            }
   522→        }
   523→        Err(e) => {
   524→            tracing::warn!("Failed to run integrity check: {e}");
   525→        }
   526→    }
   527→}
   528→```
   529→

   538→
   539→### Task 5: Create db/settings.rs — KV Store Operations
   540→
   541→Start with the simplest domain module to establish the pattern.
   542→
   543→**Files:**
   544→- Create: `src/factory/db/settings.rs`
   545→
   546→**Step 1: Write settings operations**
   547→
   548→Port from current `db.rs:1337-1367`. Change `&self` (FactoryDb) to take `conn: &Connection`. Change sync rusqlite calls to async libsql calls.
   549→
   550→Key API differences:
   551→- `rusqlite`: `conn.query_row(sql, params![key], |row| row.get(0))` (sync, closure-based)
   552→- `libsql`: `conn.query(sql, [key]).await?` then `rows.next().await?` then `row.get(0)?` (async, iterator-based)
   553→
   554→```rust
   555→use anyhow::{Context, Result};
   556→use libsql::Connection;
   557→
   558→pub async fn get_setting(conn: &Connection, key: &str) -> Result<Option<String>> {
   559→    let mut rows = conn
   560→        .query("SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = ?1", [key])
   561→        .await
   562→        .context("Failed to query setting")?;
   563→    match rows.next().await? {
   564→        Some(row) => Ok(Some(row.get::<String>(0)?)),
   565→        None => Ok(None),
   566→    }
   567→}
   568→
   569→pub async fn set_setting(conn: &Connection, key: &str, value: &str) -> Result<()> {
   570→    conn.execute(
   571→        "INSERT INTO settings (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?1, ?2, datetime('now'))
   572→         ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = ?2, updated_at = datetime('now')",
   573→        libsql::params![key, value],
   574→    )
   575→    .await
   576→    .context("Failed to set setting")?;
   577→    Ok(())
   578→}
   579→
   580→pub async fn delete_setting(conn: &Connection, key: &str) -> Result<()> {
   581→    conn.execute("DELETE FROM settings WHERE key = ?1", [key])
   582→        .await
   583→        .context("Failed to delete setting")?;
   584→    Ok(())
   585→}
   586→
   587→#[cfg(test)]
   588→mod tests {
   589→    use super::*;
   590→    use crate::factory::db::DbHandle;
   591→
   592→    #[tokio::test]
   593→    async fn test_set_and_get_setting() {
   594→        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();
   595→        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();
   596→        set_setting(&conn, "test_key", "test_value").await.unwrap();
   597→        let val = get_setting(&conn, "test_key").await.unwrap();
   598→        assert_eq!(val, Some("test_value".to_string()));
   599→    }
   600→
   601→    #[tokio::test]
   602→    async fn test_get_missing_setting() {
   603→        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();
   604→        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();
   605→        let val = get_setting(&conn, "nonexistent").await.unwrap();
   606→        assert_eq!(val, None);
   607→    }
   608→
   609→    #[tokio::test]
   610→    async fn test_upsert_setting() {
   611→        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();
   612→        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();
   613→        set_setting(&conn, "key", "v1").await.unwrap();
   614→        set_setting(&conn, "key", "v2").await.unwrap();
   615→        let val = get_setting(&conn, "key").await.unwrap();
   616→        assert_eq!(val, Some("v2".to_string()));

   618→
   619→    #[tokio::test]

   622→        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();

   625→        let val = get_setting(&conn, "key").await.unwrap();

   835→### Task 11: Update api.rs — Async Database Calls
   836→
   837→**Files:**
   838→- Modify: `src/factory/api.rs`
   839→
   840→**Step 1: Update imports**
   841→
   842→Replace:
   843→```rust
   844→use super::db::DbHandle;
   845→use super::db::FactoryDb;
   846→```
   847→With:
   848→```rust
   849→use super::db::DbHandle;
   850→```
   851→Remove `FactoryDb` import entirely — callers no longer access it directly.
   852→
   853→**Step 2: Update AppState**
   854→
   855→`AppState.db` remains `DbHandle` — no struct change needed.
   856→
   857→**Step 3: Replace all `db.call()` patterns**
   858→
   859→The old pattern:
   860→```rust
   861→let result = state.db.call(move |db| db.some_method(args)).await?;
   862→```
   863→
   864→Becomes:
   865→```rust
   866→let conn = state.db.conn()?;
   867→let result = super::db::module::some_function(&conn, args).await?;
   868→```
   869→
   870→Or if DbHandle exposes convenience methods:
   871→```rust
   872→let result = state.db.some_method(args).await?;
   873→```
   874→
   875→**Recommendation:** Add convenience methods to `DbHandle` that call `self.conn()` + domain function. This keeps callers clean:
   876→
   877→```rust
   878→// In db/mod.rs
   879→impl DbHandle {
   880→    pub async fn list_projects(&self) -> Result<Vec<Project>> {
   881→        let conn = self.conn()?;
   882→        projects::list_projects(&conn).await
   883→    }
   884→    // ... etc for all ~40 functions
   885→}
   886→```
   887→
   888→This is mechanical: for each of the ~27 `db.call()` sites in api.rs, replace with the corresponding async DbHandle method.
   889→
   890→**Step 4: Update test helpers**
   891→
   892→Replace `FactoryDb::new_in_memory()` + `DbHandle::new(db)` with `DbHandle::new_in_memory().await?`.

   966→
   967→New:

   969→use super::db::{self, DbHandle};

  1038→### Task 14: Update agent_executor.rs — Async Event Batching
  1039→
  1040→**Files:**
  1041→- Modify: `src/factory/agent_executor.rs`
  1042→
  1043→**Step 1: Replace import**
  1044→
  1045→```rust
  1046→use crate::factory::db::DbHandle;
  1047→```
  1048→
  1049→**Step 2: Replace event batching (lines 354-414)**
  1050→
  1051→Old pattern: `lock_sync()` + sync `create_agent_event()` in a loop.
  1052→
  1053→New pattern with time-based flushing:
  1054→```rust
  1055→let db_writer = self.db.clone();
  1056→let writer_task = tokio::spawn(async move {
  1057→    let mut batch = Vec::new();
  1058→    let flush_interval = Duration::from_secs(2);
  1059→
  1060→    loop {
  1061→        tokio::select! {
  1062→            event = event_rx.recv() => {
  1063→                match event {
  1064→                    Some(e) => {
  1065→                        batch.push(e);
  1066→                        // Drain additional ready events
  1067→                        while batch.len() < 25 {
  1068→                            match event_rx.try_recv() {
  1069→                                Ok(e) => batch.push(e),
  1070→                                Err(_) => break,
  1071→                            }
  1072→                        }
  1073→                        if batch.len() >= 25 {
  1074→                            flush_events(&db_writer, &mut batch).await;
  1075→                        }
  1076→                    }
  1077→                    None => {
  1078→                        // Channel closed — flush remaining and exit
  1079→                        flush_events(&db_writer, &mut batch).await;
  1080→                        break;
  1081→                    }
  1082→                }
  1083→            }
  1084→            _ = tokio::time::sleep(flush_interval) => {
  1085→                if !batch.is_empty() {
  1086→                    flush_events(&db_writer, &mut batch).await;
  1087→                }
  1088→            }
  1089→        }
  1090→    }
  1091→});
  1092→```
  1093→
  1094→Add helper:
  1095→```rust
  1096→async fn flush_events(
  1097→    db: &DbHandle,
  1098→    batch: &mut Vec<(i64, String, String, Option<serde_json::Value>)>,
  1099→) {
  1100→    for (task_id, event_type, content, metadata) in batch.drain(..) {
  1101→        if let Err(e) = db.create_agent_event(task_id, &event_type, &content, metadata.as_ref()).await {
  1102→            eprintln!("[agent] Failed to write event: {e:#}");
  1103→        }
  1104→    }
  1105→}
  1106→```
  1107→
  1108→**Step 3: Replace other db.call() sites (~4 sites)**
  1109→
  1110→```rust
  1111→// Old:
  1112→self.db.call(move |db| { db.update_agent_task_status(...) }).await?
  1113→// New:
  1114→self.db.update_agent_task_status(...).await?
  1115→```
  1116→
  1117→**Step 4: Run tests**
  1118→
  1119→Run: `cargo test factory::agent_executor --lib -- --nocapture`
  1120→
  1121→**Step 5: Commit**
  1122→
  1123→```bash
  1124→git add src/factory/agent_executor.rs
  1125→git commit -m "refactor: async event batching with time+count flush, remove lock_sync"
  1126→```

     1→//! Code Factory — Kanban board orchestration back-end.
     2→//!
     3→//! ## Overview
     4→//!
     5→//! The Factory subsystem implements a self-implementing Kanban board: issues
     6→//! are created in a SQLite database, dragged to the "In Progress" column,
     7→//! and the Factory triggers a full Forge pipeline to implement them
     8→//! autonomously, emitting real-time progress over a WebSocket.
     9→//!
    10→//! ## Module Map
    11→//!
    12→//! ```text
    13→//! ┌──────────┐   HTTP   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    14→//! │  Client  │ ───────> │  server.rs  (axum Router, ServerConfig)          │
    15→//! │  (React) │ <─────── │    └─ api.rs  (route handlers, AppState)         │
    16→//! └──────────┘ WebSocket│         │                                        │
    17→//!                       │         │ PipelineRunner::run_pipeline()          │
    18→//!                       │         v                                        │
    19→//!                       │  pipeline.rs  (PipelineRunner, GitLockMap)       │
    20→//!                       │         │                                        │
    21→//!                       │         │ Planner::plan() → Vec<AgentTask>       │
    22→//!                       │         │                                        │
    23→//!                       │         │ AgentExecutor::run_task()              │
    24→//!                       │         v                                        │
    25→//!                       │  agent_executor.rs  (TaskRunner trait)           │
    26→//!                       │         │                                        │
    27→//!                       │         │ DockerSandbox (optional isolation)     │
    28→//!                       │         v                                        │
    29→//!                       │  sandbox.rs   (DockerSandbox, SandboxConfig)     │
    30→//!                       └──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    31→//! ```
    32→//!
    33→//! ## Supporting Modules
    34→//!
    35→//! | Module           | Responsibility                                       |
    36→//! |------------------|------------------------------------------------------|
    37→//! | `models`         | Shared types: `Issue`, `AgentTask`, `IssueColumn`    |
    38→//! | `db`             | SQLite access via `DbHandle` (thin `Arc<Mutex<_>>`)  |
    39→//! | `ws`             | `WsMessage` enum + `broadcast_message()` helper      |
    40→//! | `github`         | OAuth device-flow + PR creation via `gh`             |
    41→//! | `planner`        | `Planner` trait — converts issue → `Vec<AgentTask>`  |
    42→//! | `embedded`       | Statically embeds compiled React UI (`rust-embed`)   |
    43→//!
    44→//! ## Typical Request Flow (move issue → "In Progress")
    45→//!
    46→//! 1. `PATCH /api/issues/:id/move` → `api::move_issue_handler()`
    47→//! 2. Column becomes `InProgress` → `pipeline_runner.run_pipeline(issue_id)`
    48→//! 3. `PipelineRunner` acquires a per-repo `GitLockMap` entry (prevents
    49→//!    concurrent branch checkouts on the same repo), then calls
    50→//!    `Planner::plan()` which returns a `Vec<AgentTask>`.
    51→//!    **Git branch creation** happens here: `pipeline.rs` calls
    52→//!    `git checkout -b forge/issue-<id>-<slug>` before handing tasks to the executor.
    53→//! 4. For each `AgentTask`: `AgentExecutor::run_task()` either spawns
    54→//!    the `forge` CLI directly (no sandbox) or wraps it in a
    55→//!    **Docker sandbox** (`sandbox.rs` / `DockerSandbox`) when
    56→//!    `SandboxConfig::enabled` is true. The Docker container mounts the repo
    57→//!    read-write and streams stdout lines back to the host process.
    58→//! 5. Each parsed `ParsedEvent` is persisted to `db` and broadcast via `ws`
    59→//!    so the React UI receives live phase-progress events over WebSocket.
    60→//! 6. On completion, `pipeline.rs` calls `github::create_pull_request()` (via
    61→//!    the `gh` CLI) then transitions the issue column to `Done`.
    62→
    63→pub mod agent_executor;
    64→pub mod api;
    65→pub mod db;
    66→pub mod embedded;
    67→pub mod github;
    68→pub mod models;
    69→pub mod pipeline;
    70→pub mod planner;
    71→pub mod sandbox;
    72→pub mod server;
    73→pub mod ws;
    74→
```

     1→use std::path::Path;
     2→use std::str::FromStr;
     3→use std::sync::Arc;
     4→
     5→use anyhow::{Context, Result};
     6→use rusqlite::{Connection, params};
     7→
     8→use super::models::*;
     9→
    10→/// Async-safe handle to the factory database.
    11→///
    12→/// Wraps `FactoryDb` behind `Arc<Mutex>` and runs all access on tokio's
    13→/// blocking thread pool via `spawn_blocking`, preventing synchronous SQLite
    14→/// I/O from tying up async worker threads.
    15→#[derive(Clone)]
    16→pub struct DbHandle {
    17→    inner: Arc<std::sync::Mutex<FactoryDb>>,
    18→}
    19→
    20→impl DbHandle {
    21→    pub fn new(db: FactoryDb) -> Self {
    22→        Self {
    23→            inner: Arc::new(std::sync::Mutex::new(db)),
    24→        }
    25→    }
    26→
    27→    /// Run a closure with access to the database on a blocking thread.
    28→    /// All data passed into `f` must be owned (`'static`).
    29→    pub async fn call<F, R>(&self, f: F) -> Result<R>
    30→    where
    31→        F: FnOnce(&FactoryDb) -> Result<R> + Send + 'static,
    32→        R: Send + 'static,
    33→    {
    34→        let db = self.inner.clone();
    35→        tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
    36→            let guard = db
    37→                .lock()
    38→                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("DB lock poisoned: {}", e))?;
    39→            f(&guard)
    40→        })
    41→        .await
    42→        .context("DB task panicked")?
    43→    }
    44→
    45→    /// Acquire the database mutex synchronously. Used in contexts where blocking
    46→    /// is acceptable: event batch flushing inside dedicated tasks, startup
    47→    /// initialization, and tests. Callers must ensure this is NOT called from
    48→    /// a hot async path to avoid blocking the tokio runtime.
    49→    pub fn lock_sync(&self) -> Result<std::sync::MutexGuard<'_, FactoryDb>> {
    50→        self.inner
    51→            .lock()
    52→            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("DB lock poisoned: {}", e))
    53→    }
    54→}
    55→
    56→pub struct FactoryDb {
    57→    conn: Connection,
    58→}
    59→
    60→impl FactoryDb {
    61→    /// Open (or create) a SQLite database at the given path and run migrations.
    62→    pub fn new(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
    63→        let conn = Connection::open(path).context("Failed to open SQLite database")?;
    64→        let db = Self { conn };
    65→        db.init()?;
    66→        Ok(db)
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    /// Create an in-memory SQLite database (for testing).
    70→    pub fn new_in_memory() -> Result<Self> {
    71→        let conn =
    72→            Connection::open_in_memory().context("Failed to open in-memory SQLite database")?;
    73→        let db = Self { conn };
    74→        db.init()?;
    75→        Ok(db)
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    fn init(&self) -> Result<()> {
    79→        self.conn
    80→            .execute_batch("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;")
    81→            .context("Failed to enable foreign keys")?;
    82→        self.run_migrations().context("Failed to run migrations")?;
    83→        Ok(())
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    fn run_migrations(&self) -> Result<()> {
    87→        self.conn
    88→            .execute_batch(
    89→                "
    90→                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects (
    91→                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    92→                    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    93→                    path TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    94→                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    95→                );
    96→
    97→                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS issues (
    98→                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    99→                    project_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   100→                    title TEXT NOT NULL,

   223→            "ALTER TABLE pipeline_runs ADD COLUMN team_id INTEGER REFERENCES agent_teams(id)",
   224→            [],
   225→        ) {
   226→            Ok(_) => {}
   227→            Err(e) if e.to_string().contains("duplicate column") => {}
   228→            Err(e) => return Err(anyhow::anyhow!("Failed to add team_id column: {}", e)),
   229→        }
   230→        match self.conn.execute(
   231→            "ALTER TABLE pipeline_runs ADD COLUMN has_team INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
   232→            [],
   233→        ) {
   234→            Ok(_) => {}
   235→            Err(e) if e.to_string().contains("duplicate column") => {}
   236→            Err(e) => return Err(anyhow::anyhow!("Failed to add has_team column: {}", e)),
   237→        }
   238→
   239→        // Settings key-value table
   240→        self.conn
   241→            .execute_batch(
   242→                "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings (
   243→                    key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
   244→                    value TEXT NOT NULL,
   245→                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
   246→                );",
   247→            )
   248→            .context("Failed to create settings table")?;
   249→
   250→        // Add team_id and has_team columns to pipeline_runs
   251→        let _ = self
   252→            .conn
   253→            .execute("ALTER TABLE pipeline_runs ADD COLUMN team_id INTEGER", []);
   254→        let _ = self.conn.execute(
   255→            "ALTER TABLE pipeline_runs ADD COLUMN has_team INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
   256→            [],
   257→        );
   258→
   259→        Ok(())
   260→    }
   261→
   262→    // ── Project CRUD ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   263→
   264→    pub fn create_project(&self, name: &str, path: &str) -> Result<Project> {
   265→        self.conn
   266→            .execute(
   267→                "INSERT INTO projects (name, path) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
   268→                params![name, path],
   269→            )
   270→            .context("Failed to insert project")?;
   271→        let id = self.conn.last_insert_rowid();
   272→        self.get_project(id)?
   273→            .context("Project not found after insert")
   274→    }
   275→
   276→    pub fn list_projects(&self) -> Result<Vec<Project>> {
   277→        let mut stmt = self
   278→            .conn
   279→            .prepare("SELECT id, name, path, github_repo, created_at FROM projects ORDER BY id")
   280→            .context("Failed to prepare list_projects")?;
   281→        let rows = stmt
   282→            .query_map([], |row| {
   283→                Ok(Project {
   284→                    id: row.get(0)?,
   285→                    name: row.get(1)?,
   286→                    path: row.get(2)?,
   287→                    github_repo: row.get(3)?,
   288→                    created_at: row.get(4)?,
   289→                })
   290→            })
   291→            .context("Failed to query projects")?;
   292→        let mut projects = Vec::new();
   293→        for row in rows {
   294→            projects.push(row.context("Failed to read project row")?);
   295→        }
   296→        Ok(projects)
   297→    }

    22→/// Configuration for the factory server.
    23→pub struct ServerConfig {
    24→    pub port: u16,
    25→    pub db_path: std::path::PathBuf,
    26→    pub project_path: String,
    27→    pub dev_mode: bool,
    28→}
    29→
    30→impl Default for ServerConfig {
    31→    fn default() -> Self {
    32→        Self {
    33→            port: 3141,
    34→            db_path: std::path::PathBuf::from(".forge/factory.db"),
    35→            project_path: ".".to_string(),
    36→            dev_mode: false,
    37→        }
    38→    }
    39→}
    40→
    41→/// Build the full application router with API, WebSocket, and SPA serving.
    42→pub fn build_router(state: Arc<AppState>) -> Router {
    43→    let ws_tx = state.ws_tx.clone();
    44→
    45→    api::api_router()
    46→        .route(
    47→            "/ws",
    48→            get(move |ws_upgrade| ws::ws_handler_with_sender(ws_upgrade, ws_tx)),
    49→        )
    50→        .fallback(static_handler)
    51→        .with_state(state)
    52→}
    53→
    54→/// Serve embedded static files or fall back to index.html for SPA routing.
    55→async fn static_handler(req: Request<Body>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    56→    let path = req.uri().path().trim_start_matches('/');
    57→
    58→    // Try to serve the exact file
    59→    if !path.is_empty()
    60→        && let Some(content) = Assets::get(path)
    61→    {
    62→        let mime = mime_guess::from_path(path).first_or_octet_stream();
    63→        match Response::builder()
    64→            .header(header::CONTENT_TYPE, mime.as_ref())
    65→            .body(Body::from(content.data.to_vec()))
    66→        {
    67→            Ok(resp) => return resp.into_response(),
    68→            Err(_) => return StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR.into_response(),
    69→        }
    70→    }
    71→
    72→    // Fall back to index.html for SPA client-side routing
    73→    match Assets::get("index.html") {
    74→        Some(content) => Html(String::from_utf8_lossy(&content.data).to_string()).into_response(),
    75→        None => (
    76→            StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
    77→            "Frontend not found. Run 'npm run build' in ui/ directory.",
    78→        )
    79→            .into_response(),
    80→    }
    81→}
    82→
    83→/// Start the factory server.
    84→pub async fn start_server(config: ServerConfig) -> Result<()> {
    85→    // Ensure parent directory exists for DB
    86→    if let Some(parent) = config.db_path.parent() {
    87→        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).context("Failed to create database directory")?;
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    let db = FactoryDb::new(&config.db_path).context("Failed to initialize factory database")?;
    91→    let (ws_tx, _rx) = broadcast::channel::<String>(256);
    92→    let github_client_id = std::env::var("GITHUB_CLIENT_ID").ok();
    93→
    94→    // Check if Docker sandboxing is enabled
    95→    let sandbox = if std::env::var("FORGE_SANDBOX").unwrap_or_default() == "true" {
    96→        match DockerSandbox::new("forge:local".to_string()).await {
    97→            Some(sandbox) => {
    98→                eprintln!("[factory] Docker sandbox enabled");
    99→                let s = Arc::new(sandbox);
   100→                if let Ok(pruned) = s.prune_stale_containers(7200).await
   101→                    && pruned > 0
   102→                {
   103→                    eprintln!("[factory] Pruned {} stale pipeline containers", pruned);
   104→                }
   105→                Some(s)
   106→            }
   107→            None => {
   108→                eprintln!(
   109→                    "[factory] FORGE_SANDBOX=true but Docker is not available, falling back to local execution"
   110→                );
   111→                None
   112→            }
   113→        }
   114→    } else {
   115→        None
   116→    };
   117→
   118→    let pipeline_runner = PipelineRunner::new(&config.project_path, sandbox);
   119→    let db_handle = DbHandle::new(db);
   120→
   121→    // Recover orphaned runs from a previous server instance
   122→    match db_handle.lock_sync() {
   123→        Ok(db) => match db.recover_orphaned_runs() {
   124→            Ok(0) => {}
   125→            Ok(n) => eprintln!(
   126→                "[factory] Recovered {} orphaned pipeline run(s) from previous session",
   127→                n
   128→            ),
   129→            Err(e) => eprintln!("[factory] Warning: failed to recover orphaned runs: {}", e),
   130→        },
   131→        Err(e) => eprintln!(
   132→            "[factory] Warning: could not acquire DB lock for orphan recovery: {}",
   133→            e
   134→        ),
   135→    }
   136→
   137→    let persisted_token = db_handle
   138→        .lock_sync()
   139→        .context("Failed to acquire DB lock during startup")?
   140→        .get_setting("github_token")
   141→        .ok()
   142→        .flatten();
   143→
   144→    let state = Arc::new(AppState {
   145→        db: db_handle,
   146→        ws_tx,
   147→        pipeline_runner,
   148→        github_client_id,
   149→        github_token: std::sync::Mutex::new(persisted_token),
   150→    });
   151→
   152→    let state_for_shutdown = Arc::clone(&state);
   153→    let mut app = build_router(state);
   154→
   155→    if config.dev_mode {
   156→        app = app.layer(CorsLayer::permissive());
   157→    }
   158→
   159→    let host = if config.dev_mode {
   160→        "0.0.0.0"
   161→    } else {
   162→        "127.0.0.1"
   163→    };
   164→    let addr = format!("{}:{}", host, config.port);
   165→    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(&addr)
   166→        .await
   167→        .with_context(|| format!("Failed to bind to {}", addr))?;
   168→
   169→    let local_addr = listener.local_addr()?;
   170→    println!("Forge Factory running at http://{}", local_addr);
   171→
   172→    axum::serve(listener, app)
   173→        .with_graceful_shutdown(shutdown_signal())
   174→        .await
   175→        .context("Server error")?;
   176→
   177→    // Stop all active pipeline containers/processes
   178→    state_for_shutdown.pipeline_runner.shutdown().await;
   179→
   180→    println!("Server shut down gracefully.");
   181→    Ok(())
   182→}
   183→
   184→async fn shutdown_signal() {
   185→    tokio::signal::ctrl_c()
   186→        .await
   187→        .expect("Failed to install Ctrl+C handler");
   188→    println!("\nShutting down...");
   189→}

     1→use anyhow::{Context, Result};
     2→use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
     3→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
     4→use std::fs;
     5→use std::io::Write;
     6→
     7→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
     8→pub struct StateEntry {
     9→    pub phase: String,
    10→    /// Sub-phase number if this is a sub-phase entry (e.g., "05.1")
    11→    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    12→    pub sub_phase: Option<String>,
    13→    pub iteration: u32,
    14→    pub status: String,
    15→    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,
    16→}
    17→
    18→impl StateEntry {

}

### Apply sqld incremental snapshots to a local libSQL replica in Rust

Source: https://github.com/tursodatabase/libsql/blob/main/docs/USER_GUIDE.md

A Rust program demonstrating how to apply an incremental snapshot file to a local `libsql` database replica. It uses `libsql` and `libsql_replication` crates to open a database, load a snapshot from a file, and synchronize frames.

```rust
use libsql::Database;
use libsql_replication::{Frames, TempSnapshot};

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() {
    tracing_subscriber::fmt::init();

    let opts = libsql::Opts::with_sync();
    let db = Database::open_with_opts("test.db", opts).await.unwrap();
    let conn = db.connect().unwrap();

    let args = std::env::args().collect::<Vec<String>>();
    if args.len() < 2 {
        println!("Usage: {} <snapshot path>", args[0]);
        return;
    }
    let snapshot_path = args.get(1).unwrap();
    let snapshot = TempSnapshot::from_snapshot_file(snapshot_path.as_ref()).unwrap();

    db.sync_frames(Frames::Snapshot(snapshot)).unwrap();

    let rows = conn
        .query("SELECT * FROM sqlite_master", ())
        .unwrap()
        .unwrap();
    while let Ok(Some(row)) = rows.next() {
        println!(
            "| {:024} | {:024} | {:024} | {:024} |",
            row.get::<&str>(0).unwrap(),
            row.get::<&str>(1).unwrap(),
            row.get::<&str>(2).unwrap(),
            row.get::<&str>(3).unwrap(),
        );
    }
}
```

--------------------------------

### Feature: query_map Function (v0.1.0)

Source: https://github.com/tursodatabase/libsql/blob/main/vendored/rusqlite/Changelog.md

Adds the `query_map` function, providing a convenient way to execute a query and map each resulting row to a Rust type using a provided closure.

```APIDOC
New Function:
  - `query_map`
  - Purpose: Execute a query and map each row to a Rust type.
```

--------------------------------

### APIDOC: Statement::query_row Convenience Function

Source: https://github.com/tursodatabase/libsql/blob/main/vendored/rusqlite/Changelog.md

A new `query_row` convenience function has been added to the `Statement` struct. This provides a simpler way to execute queries that are expected to return a single row, reducing boilerplate.

```APIDOC
Statement::query_row<F, T>(&self, params: &[&ToSql], f: F) -> Result<T, Error>
where F: FnOnce(&Row) -> Result<T, Error>
```

--------------------------------

### Perform Basic SQLite Operations in Rust with Rusqlite

Source: https://github.com/tursodatabase/libsql/blob/main/vendored/rusqlite/README.md

This Rust example demonstrates fundamental database operations using `rusqlite`. It shows how to open an in-memory SQLite connection, create a table, insert data, and query data. It also illustrates how to map query results to a custom Rust struct (`Person`) using `query_map`.

```Rust
use rusqlite::{Connection, Result};

#[derive(Debug)]
struct Person {
    id: i32,
    name: String,
    data: Option<Vec<u8>>,
}

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    let conn = Connection::open_in_memory()?;

    conn.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE person (
            id    INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            name  TEXT NOT NULL,
            data  BLOB
        )",
        (), // empty list of parameters.
    )?;
    let me = Person {
        id: 0,
        name: "Steven".to_string(),
        data: None,
    };
    conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO person (name, data) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
        (&me.name, &me.data),
    )?;

    let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT id, name, data FROM person")?;
    let person_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
        Ok(Person {
            id: row.get(0)?,
            name: row.get(1)?,
            data: row.get(2)?,
        })
    })?;

    for person in person_iter {
        println!("Found person {:?}", person.unwrap());
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

--------------------------------

### APIDOC: Transaction Creation Mutability Change

Source: https://github.com/tursodatabase/libsql/blob/main/vendored/rusqlite/Changelog.md

Creating transactions from a `Connection` or savepoints from a `Transaction` now requires `&mut self`. This correctly represents the inherent nesting of transactions and makes the parent unusable while a transaction is active. `Transaction` now implements `Deref<Target=Connection>` for method access.

```APIDOC
Connection::transaction(&mut self, ...) -> Result<Transaction, Error>
Transaction::savepoint(&mut self, ...) -> Result<Savepoint, Error>

impl Deref for Transaction {
  type Target = Connection;
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jdsingh/Projects/AI/forge/src/factory/db/migrations.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse libsql::Connection;\n\nconst MIGRATIONS: &[(i64, &str)] = &[\n    (1, include_str!(\"migrations/001_initial.sql\")),\n    (2, include_str!(\"migrations/002_github_integration.sql\")),\n    (3, include_str!(\"migrations/003_agent_teams.sql\")),\n    (4, include_str!(\"migrations/004_settings.sql\")),\n    (5, include_str!(\"migrations/005_orchestrator_state.sql\")),\n    (6, include_str!(\"migrations/006_soft_deletes_and_indexes.sql\")),\n];\n\n/// Run all pending migrations. Uses PRAGMA user_version to track state.\npub async fn run_migrations(conn: &Connection) -> Result<()> {\n    let current_version = get_user_version(conn).await?;\n\n    // Bootstrap: if tables exist but user_version is 0 (pre-migration DB),\n    // detect existing schema and set version accordingly.\n    if current_version == 0 {\n        let bootstrapped = bootstrap_existing_db(conn).await?;\n        if bootstrapped > 0 {\n            tracing::info!(\"Bootstrapped existing database at migration version {bootstrapped}\");\n            return run_from_version(conn, bootstrapped).await;\n        }\n    }\n\n    run_from_version(conn, current_version).await\n}\n\nasync fn run_from_version(conn: &Connection, current: i64) -> Result<()> {\n    for (version, sql) in MIGRATIONS.iter() {\n        if *version <= current {\n            continue;\n        }\n        tracing::info!(\"Running migration {version}...\");\n        conn.execute_batch(sql)\n            .await\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"Failed to run migration {version}\"))?;\n        set_user_version(conn, *version).await?;\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nasync fn get_user_version(conn: &Connection) -> Result<i64> {\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(\"PRAGMA user_version\", ())\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to query user_version\")?;\n    match rows.next().await? {\n        Some(row) => Ok(row.get::<i64>(0)?),\n        None => Ok(0),\n    }\n}\n\nasync fn set_user_version(conn: &Connection, version: i64) -> Result<()> {\n    conn.execute(&format!(\"PRAGMA user_version = {version}\"), ())\n        .await\n        .with_context(|| format!(\"Failed to set user_version to {version}\"))?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Detect if this is a pre-migration database (tables exist but user_version=0).\n/// Returns the migration version that matches the current schema, or 0 if empty.\nasync fn bootstrap_existing_db(conn: &Connection) -> Result<i64> {\n    // Check if the core 'projects' table exists\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(\n            \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='projects'\",\n            (),\n        )\n        .await?;\n\n    if rows.next().await?.is_none() {\n        return Ok(0); // Fresh database\n    }\n\n    // Tables exist — determine how far the schema has evolved.\n    // Check for settings table (migration 4)\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(\n            \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='settings'\",\n            (),\n        )\n        .await?;\n    if rows.next().await?.is_some() {\n        set_user_version(conn, 4).await?;\n        return Ok(4);\n    }\n\n    // Check for agent_teams table (migration 3)\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(\n            \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='agent_teams'\",\n            (),\n        )\n        .await?;\n    if rows.next().await?.is_some() {\n        set_user_version(conn, 3).await?;\n        return Ok(3);\n    }\n\n    // Check for github_repo column (migration 2)\n    let mut rows = conn.query(\"PRAGMA table_info(projects)\", ()).await?;\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next().await? {\n        let name: String = row.get(1)?;\n        if name == \"github_repo\" {\n            set_user_version(conn, 2).await?;\n            return Ok(2);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Base tables only (migration 1)\n    set_user_version(conn, 1).await?;\n    Ok(1)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use libsql::Builder;\n\n    async fn test_db() -> (libsql::Database, Connection) {\n        let db = Builder::new_local(\":memory:\").build().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.connect().unwrap();\n        (db, conn)\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_fresh_database_runs_all_migrations() {\n        let (_db, conn) = test_db().await;\n        run_migrations(&conn).await.unwrap();\n        let version = get_user_version(&conn).await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(version, 6);\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_idempotent_migration() {\n        let (_db, conn) = test_db().await;\n        run_migrations(&conn).await.unwrap();\n        // Running again should be a no-op\n        run_migrations(&conn).await.unwrap();\n        let version = get_user_version(&conn).await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(version, 6);\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_partial_migration_resumes() {\n        let (_db, conn) = test_db().await;\n        // Run only first 2 migrations manually\n        conn.execute_batch(MIGRATIONS[0].1).await.unwrap();\n        conn.execute_batch(MIGRATIONS[1].1).await.unwrap();\n        set_user_version(&conn, 2).await.unwrap();\n\n        // Now run_migrations should pick up from 3\n        run_migrations(&conn).await.unwrap();\n        let version = get_user_version(&conn).await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(version, 6);\n    }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jdsingh/Projects/AI/forge/src/factory/db/mod.rs",
  "content": "pub mod agents;\npub mod issues;\npub mod migrations;\npub mod pipeline;\npub mod projects;\npub mod settings;\n\nuse std::path::Path;\nuse std::time::Duration;\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse libsql::{Builder, Connection, Database};\n\n/// Async database handle wrapping a libsql Database.\n///\n/// Supports two modes:\n/// - **Turso embedded replica** when TURSO_DATABASE_URL and TURSO_AUTH_TOKEN are set\n/// - **Local SQLite** otherwise (dev/offline fallback)\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct DbHandle {\n    db: Database,\n}\n\nimpl DbHandle {\n    /// Open a local-only SQLite database at the given path.\n    pub async fn new_local(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {\n        let path_str = path.to_string_lossy().to_string();\n        let db = Builder::new_local(&path_str)\n            .build()\n            .await\n            .context(\"Failed to open local SQLite database\")?;\n        let handle = Self { db };\n        handle.init().await?;\n        Ok(handle)\n    }\n\n    /// Open a Turso embedded replica with local file cache.\n    pub async fn new_remote_replica(\n        local_path: &Path,\n        url: &str,\n        token: &str,\n    ) -> Result<Self> {\n        let path_str = local_path.to_string_lossy().to_string();\n        let db = Builder::new_remote_replica(\n            &path_str,\n            url.to_string(),\n            token.to_string(),\n        )\n        .sync_interval(Duration::from_secs(60))\n        .read_your_writes(true)\n        .build()\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to open Turso embedded replica\")?;\n        let handle = Self { db };\n        handle.init().await?;\n        Ok(handle)\n    }\n\n    /// Open an in-memory database (for testing).\n    pub async fn new_in_memory() -> Result<Self> {\n        let db = Builder::new_local(\":memory:\")\n            .build()\n            .await\n            .context(\"Failed to open in-memory database\")?;\n        let handle = Self { db };\n        handle.init().await?;\n        Ok(handle)\n    }\n\n    /// Get a new connection from the database.\n    pub fn conn(&self) -> Result<Connection> {\n        self.db\n            .connect()\n            .context(\"Failed to get database connection\")\n    }\n\n    /// Sync embedded replica with remote (no-op for local-only).\n    pub async fn sync(&self) -> Result<()> {\n        // sync() returns a result with replication info; we just need it to succeed\n        let _ = self.db.sync().await;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Initialize pragmas and run migrations.\n    async fn init(&self) -> Result<()> {\n        let conn = self.conn()?;\n        init_pragmas(&conn).await?;\n        migrations::run_migrations(&conn).await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Set WAL mode, busy timeout, and enable foreign keys.\nasync fn init_pragmas(conn: &Connection) -> Result<()> {\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL\", ())\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to set WAL mode\")?;\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000\", ())\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to set busy_timeout\")?;\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON\", ())\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to enable foreign keys\")?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Run integrity check on startup. Logs warning but does not fail.\npub async fn health_check(conn: &Connection) {\n    match conn.query(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\", ()).await {\n        Ok(mut rows) => {\n            if let Ok(Some(row)) = rows.next().await {\n                if let Ok(result) = row.get::<String>(0) {\n                    if result != \"ok\" {\n                        tracing::warn!(\"Database integrity check failed: {result}\");\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Err(e) => {\n            tracing::warn!(\"Failed to run integrity check: {e}\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jdsingh/Projects/AI/forge/src/factory/db/settings.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse libsql::Connection;\n\npub async fn get_setting(conn: &Connection, key: &str) -> Result<Option<String>> {\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(\"SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = ?1\", [key])\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to query setting\")?;\n    match rows.next().await? {\n        Some(row) => Ok(Some(row.get::<String>(0)?)),\n        None => Ok(None),\n    }\n}\n\npub async fn set_setting(conn: &Connection, key: &str, value: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO settings (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?1, ?2, datetime('now'))\n         ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value, updated_at = datetime('now')\",\n        (key, value),\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to upsert setting\")?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\npub async fn delete_setting(conn: &Connection, key: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM settings WHERE key = ?1\", [key])\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to delete setting\")?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::factory::db::DbHandle;\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_set_and_get_setting() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        set_setting(&conn, \"test_key\", \"test_value\").await.unwrap();\n        let val = get_setting(&conn, \"test_key\").await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(val, Some(\"test_value\".to_string()));\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_get_missing_setting() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        let val = get_setting(&conn, \"nonexistent\").await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(val, None);\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_upsert_setting() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        set_setting(&conn, \"key\", \"v1\").await.unwrap();\n        set_setting(&conn, \"key\", \"v2\").await.unwrap();\n        let val = get_setting(&conn, \"key\").await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(val, Some(\"v2\".to_string()));\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_delete_setting() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        set_setting(&conn, \"key\", \"value\").await.unwrap();\n        delete_setting(&conn, \"key\").await.unwrap();\n        let val = get_setting(&conn, \"key\").await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(val, None);\n    }\n}\n"
}

   332→    pub fn delete_project(&self, id: i64) -> Result<bool> {
   333→        // Manually clean up agent tables that lack ON DELETE CASCADE.
   334→        // Chain: project -> issues -> pipeline_runs -> agent_teams -> agent_tasks -> agent_events
   335→        self.conn
   336→            .execute(
   337→                "DELETE FROM agent_events WHERE task_id IN (
   338→                    SELECT t.id FROM agent_tasks t
   339→                    JOIN agent_teams at ON t.team_id = at.id
   340→                    JOIN pipeline_runs r ON at.run_id = r.id
   341→                    JOIN issues i ON r.issue_id = i.id
   342→                    WHERE i.project_id = ?1
   343→                )",
   344→                params![id],
   345→            )
   346→            .context("Failed to delete agent events for project")?;
   347→        self.conn
   348→            .execute(
   349→                "DELETE FROM agent_tasks WHERE team_id IN (
   350→                    SELECT at.id FROM agent_teams at
   351→                    JOIN pipeline_runs r ON at.run_id = r.id
   352→                    JOIN issues i ON r.issue_id = i.id
   353→                    WHERE i.project_id = ?1
   354→                )",
   355→                params![id],
   356→            )
   357→            .context("Failed to delete agent tasks for project")?;
   358→        // Null out the back-reference from pipeline_runs.team_id before removing teams
   359→        self.conn
   360→            .execute(
   361→                "UPDATE pipeline_runs SET team_id = NULL WHERE issue_id IN (
   362→                    SELECT i.id FROM issues i WHERE i.project_id = ?1
   363→                )",
   364→                params![id],
   365→            )
   366→            .context("Failed to clear team_id on pipeline runs")?;
   367→        self.conn
   368→            .execute(
   369→                "DELETE FROM agent_teams WHERE run_id IN (

name: &str, path: &str) -> Result<Project> {\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO projects (name, path) VALUES (?1, ?2)\",\n        (name, path),\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to insert project\")?;\n    let id = conn.last_insert_rowid();\n    get_project(conn, id)\n        .await?\n        .context(\"Project not found after insert\")\n}\n\npub async fn list_projects(conn:          (),\n        )\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to query projects\")?;\n    let mut projects = Vec::new();\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next().await? {\n delete_project(conn: &Connection, id: i64) -> Result<bool> {\n    // Manually clean up agent tables that lack ON DELETE CASCADE.\n    // Chain: project -> issues -> pipeline_runs -> agent_teams -> agent_tasks -> agent_events\n    conn.execute(\n        \"DELETE FROM agent_events WHERE task_id IN (\n            SELECT t.id FROM agent_tasks t\n            JOIN agent_teams at ON t.team_id = at.id\n            JOIN pipeline_runs r ON at.run_id = r.id\n            JOIN issues i ON r.issue_id = i.id\n            WHERE i.project_id = ?1\n        )\",\n        [id],\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to delete agent events for project\")?;\n    conn.execute(\n        \"DELETE FROM agent_tasks WHERE team_id IN (\n            SELECT at.id FROM agent_teams at\n            JOIN pipeline_runs r ON at.run_id = r.id\n            JOIN issues i ON r.issue_id = i.id\n            WHERE i.project_id = ?1\n        )\",\n        [id],\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to delete agent tasks for project\")?;\n    // Null out the back-reference from pipeline_runs.team_id before removing teams\n    conn.execute(\n        \"UPDATE pipeline_runs SET team_id = NULL WHERE issue_id IN (\n            SELECT i.id FROM issues i WHERE i.project_id = ?1\n        )\",\n        [id],\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to clear team_id on pipeline runs\")?;\n    conn.execute(\n        \"DELETE FROM agent_teams WHERE run_id IN (\n            SELECT r.id FROM pipeline_runs r\n            JOIN issues i ON r.issue_id = i.id\n            WHERE i.project_id = ?1\n        )\",\n        [id],\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to delete agent teams for project\")?;\n\n    let count = conn\n        .execute(\"DELETE FROM projects WHERE id = ?1\", [id])\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to delete project\")?;\n    Ok(count > 0)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::factory::db::DbHandle;\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_create_project() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n\n        let project = create_project(&conn, \"my-project\", > 0);\n        assert!(!project.created_at.is_empty());\n\n        let fetched = get_project(&conn, project.id)\n            .await\n            .unwrap()\n            .expect(\"project should exist\");\n        assert_eq!(fetched.name, \"my-project\");\n        assert_eq!(fetched.path, \"/tmp/my-project\");\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_list_projects() {\n        let db = \"/tmp/beta\").await.unwrap();\n        create_project(&conn, \"gamma\", \"/tmp/gamma\").await.unwrap();\n\n        let projects = list_projects(&conn).await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(projects.len(), 3);\n        assert_eq!(projects[0].name, \"alpha\");\n        assert_eq!(projects[1].name, \"beta\");\n        assert_eq!(projects[2].name, \"gamma\");\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_delete_project() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n\n        let project = create_project(&conn, \"to-delete\", \"/tmp/to-delete\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let deleted = delete_project(&conn, project.id).await.unwrap();\n        assert!(deleted);\n\n        let fetched = get_project(&conn, project.id).await.unwrap();\n        // Hard delete, so it should be gone\n        assert!(fetched.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_update_github_repo() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n\n        let project = create_project(&conn, \"gh-proj\", \"/tmp/gh-proj\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        assert!(project.github_repo.is_none());\n\n        let updated =\n            update_project_github_repo(&conn, project.id, \"owner/repo\")\n                .await\n                .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(updated.github_repo, Some(\"owner/repo\".to_string()));\n    }\n}\n"

   385→    // ── Issue CRUD ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   386→
   387→    pub fn create_issue(
   388→        &self,
   389→        project_id: i64,
   390→        title: &str,
   391→        description: &str,
   392→        column: &IssueColumn,
   393→    ) -> Result<Issue> {
   394→        // Determine the next position in the target column for this project.
   395→        let max_pos: i32 = self
   396→            .conn
   397→            .query_row(
   398→                "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(position), -1) FROM issues WHERE project_id = ?1 AND column_name = ?2",
   399→                params![project_id, column.as_str()],
   400→                |row| row.get(0),
   401→            )
   402→            .context("Failed to get max position")?;
   403→        let position = max_pos + 1;
   404→
   405→        self.conn
   406→            .execute(
   407→                "INSERT INTO issues (project_id, title, description, column_name, position) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5)",
   408→                params![project_id, title, description, column.as_str(), position],
   409→            )
   410→            .context("Failed to insert issue")?;
   411→        let id = self.conn.last_insert_rowid();
   412→        self.get_issue(id)?.context("Issue not found after insert")
   413→    }
   414→
   415→    pub fn list_issues(&self, project_id: i64) -> Result<Vec<Issue>> {
   416→        let mut stmt = self
   417→            .conn
   418→            .prepare(
   419→                "SELECT id, project_id, title, description, column_name, position, priority, labels, github_issue_number, created_at, updated_at
   420→                 FROM issues WHERE project_id = ?1 ORDER BY position",
   421→            )
   422→            .context("Failed to prepare list_issues")?;
   423→        let rows = stmt
   424→            .query_map(params![project_id], |row| {
   425→                Ok(IssueRow {
   426→                    id: row.get(0)?,
   427→                    project_id: row.get(1)?,
   428→                    title: row.get(2)?,
   429→                    description: row.get(3)?,
   430→                    column_name: row.get(4)?,
   431→                    position: row.get(5)?,
   432→                    priority: row.get(6)?,
   433→                    labels: row.get(7)?,
   434→                    github_issue_number: row.get(8)?,
   435→                    created_at: row.get(9)?,
   436→                    updated_at: row.get(10)?,
   437→                })
   438→            })
   439→            .context("Failed to query issues")?;
   440→        let mut issues = Vec::new();
   441→        for row in rows {
   442→            let r = row.context("Failed to read issue row")?;
   443→            issues.push(r.into_issue()?);
   444→        }
   445→        Ok(issues)
   446→    }
   447→
   448→    pub fn get_issue(&self, id: i64) -> Result<Option<Issue>> {
   449→        let mut stmt = self
   450→            .conn
   451→            .prepare(
   452→                "SELECT id, project_id, title, description, column_name, position, priority, labels, github_issue_number, created_at, updated_at
   453→                 FROM issues WHERE id = ?1",
   454→            )
   455→            .context("Failed to prepare get_issue")?;
   456→        let mut rows = stmt
   457→            .query_map(params![id], |row| {
   458→                Ok(IssueRow {
   459→                    id: row.get(0)?,
   460→                    project_id: row.get(1)?,
   461→                    title: row.get(2)?,
   462→                    description: row.get(3)?,
   463→                    column_name: row.get(4)?,
   464→                    position: row.get(5)?,
   465→                    priority: row.get(6)?,
   466→                    labels: row.get(7)?,
   467→                    github_issue_number: row.get(8)?,
   468→                    created_at: row.get(9)?,
   469→                    updated_at: row.get(10)?,
   470→                })
   471→            })
   472→            .context("Failed to query issue")?;
   473→        match rows.next() {
   474→            Some(row) => {
   475→                let r = row.context("Failed to read issue row")?;
   476→                Ok(Some(r.into_issue()?))
   477→            }
   478→            None => Ok(None),
   479→        }
   480→    }
   481→
   482→    pub fn update_issue(
   483→        &self,
   484→        id: i64,
   485→        title: Option<&str>,
   486→        description: Option<&str>,
   487→        priority: Option<&str>,
   488→        labels: Option<&str>,
   489→    ) -> Result<Issue> {
   490→        // Validate priority before touching the DB.
   491→        if let Some(p) = priority {
   492→            Priority::from_str(p)
   493→                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))
   494→                .context("Invalid priority value")?;
   495→        }
   496→
   497→        // Use unchecked_transaction so all updates are atomic.
   498→        // Safety: DbHandle's Mutex already guarantees single-threaded access.
   499→        let tx = self
   500→            .conn
   501→            .unchecked_transaction()
   502→            .context("Failed to begin transaction")?;
   503→
   504→        if let Some(t) = title {
   505→            tx.execute(
   506→                "UPDATE issues SET title = ?1, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?2",
   507→                params![t, id],
   508→            )
   509→            .context("Failed to update issue title")?;
   510→        }
   511→        if let Some(d) = description {
   512→            tx.execute(
   513→                "UPDATE issues SET description = ?1, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?2",
   514→                params![d, id],
   515→            )
   516→            .context("Failed to update issue description")?;
   517→        }
   518→        if let Some(p) = priority {
   519→            tx.execute(
   520→                "UPDATE issues SET priority = ?1, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?2",
   521→                params![p, id],
   522→            )
   523→            .context("Failed to update issue priority")?;
   524→        }
   525→        if let Some(l) = labels {
   526→            tx.execute(
   527→                "UPDATE issues SET labels = ?1, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?2",
   528→                params![l, id],
   529→            )
   530→            .context("Failed to update issue labels")?;
   531→        }
   532→
   533→        tx.commit().context("Failed to commit issue update")?;
   534→        self.get_issue(id)?.context("Issue not found after update")
   535→    }
   536→
   537→    pub fn move_issue(&self, id: i64, column: &IssueColumn, position: i32) -> Result<Issue> {
   538→        self.conn
   539→            .execute(
   540→                "UPDATE issues SET column_name = ?1, position = ?2, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?3",
   541→                params![column.as_str(), position, id],
   542→            )
   543→            .context("Failed to move issue")?;
   544→        self.get_issue(id)?.context("Issue not found after move")
   545→    }
   546→
   547→    pub fn delete_issue(&self, id: i64) -> Result<bool> {
   548→        let count = self
   549→            .conn
   550→            .execute("DELETE FROM issues WHERE id = ?1", params![id])
   551→            .context("Failed to delete issue")?;
   552→        Ok(count > 0)
   553→    }
   554→
   555→    pub fn create_issue_from_github(
   556→        &self,
   557→        project_id: i64,
   558→        title: &str,
   559→        description: &str,
   560→        github_issue_number: i64,
   561→    ) -> Result<Option<Issue>> {
   562→        let exists: bool = self.conn.query_row(
   563→            "SELECT COUNT(*) > 0 FROM issues WHERE project_id = ?1 AND github_issue_number = ?2",
   564→            params![project_id, github_issue_number],
   565→            |row| row.get(0),
   566→        )?;
   567→        if exists {
   568→            return Ok(None);
   569→        }
   570→
   571→        let max_pos: i32 = self.conn.query_row(
   572→            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(position), -1) FROM issues WHERE project_id = ?1 AND column_name = 'backlog'",
   573→            params![project_id],
   574→            |row| row.get(0),
   575→        )?;
   576→
   577→        self.conn.execute(
   578→            "INSERT INTO issues (project_id, title, description, column_name, position, github_issue_number)
   579→             VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, 'backlog', ?4, ?5)",
   580→            params![project_id, title, description, max_pos + 1, github_issue_number],
   581→        ).context("Failed to insert github issue")?;
   582→        let id = self.conn.last_insert_rowid();
   583→        self.get_issue(id)
   584→    }
   585→
   586→    // ── Board view ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   587→
   588→    pub fn get_board(&self, project_id: i64) -> Result<BoardView> {
   589→        let project = self
   590→            .get_project(project_id)?
   591→            .context("Project not found for board view")?;
   592→
   593→        let all_issues = self.list_issues(project_id)?;
   594→
   595→        let column_order = [
   596→            IssueColumn::Backlog,
   597→            IssueColumn::Ready,
   598→            IssueColumn::InProgress,
   599→            IssueColumn::InReview,
   600→            IssueColumn::Done,
   601→        ];
   602→
   603→        let mut columns = Vec::new();
   604→        for col in &column_order {
   605→            let mut col_issues: Vec<IssueWithStatus> = all_issues
   606→                .iter()
   607→                .filter(|i| i.column == *col)
   608→                .map(|i| IssueWithStatus {
   609→                    issue: i.clone(),
   610→                    active_run: None,
   611→                })
   612→                .collect();
   613→
   614→            // Sort by position
   615→            col_issues.sort_by_key(|iws| iws.issue.position);
   616→
   617→            // Find active runs for each issue
   618→            for iws in &mut col_issues {
   619→                let mut stmt = self.conn.prepare(
   620→                    "SELECT id, issue_id, status, phase_count, current_phase, iteration, summary, error, branch_name, pr_url, team_id, has_team, started_at, completed_at
   621→                     FROM pipeline_runs
   622→                     WHERE issue_id = ?1 AND status IN ('queued', 'running')
   623→                     ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",
   624→                )?;
   625→                let mut rows = stmt.query_map(params![iws.issue.id], |row| {
   626→                    Ok(PipelineRunRow {
   627→                        id: row.get(0)?,
   628→                        issue_id: row.get(1)?,
   629→                        status: row.get(2)?,
   630→                        phase_count: row.get(3)?,
   631→                        current_phase: row.get(4)?,
   632→                        iteration: row.get(5)?,
   633→                        summary: row.get(6)?,
   634→                        error: row.get(7)?,
   635→                        branch_name: row.get(8)?,
   636→                        pr_url: row.get(9)?,
   637→                        team_id: row.get(10)?,
   638→                        has_team: row.get(11)?,
   639→                        started_at: row.get(12)?,
   640→                        completed_at: row.get(13)?,
   641→                    })
   642→                })?;
   643→                if let Some(row) = rows.next() {
   644→                    let r = row.context("Failed to read pipeline_run row")?;
   645→                    iws.active_run = Some(r.into_pipeline_run()?);
   646→                }
   647→            }
   648→
   649→            columns.push(ColumnView {
   650→                name: col.clone(),
   651→                issues: col_issues,
   652→            });
   653→        }
   654→
   655→        Ok(BoardView { project, columns })
   656→    }
   657→
   658→    // ── Issue detail ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   659→
   660→    pub fn get_issue_detail(&self, id: i64) -> Result<Option<IssueDetail>> {
   661→        let issue = match self.get_issue(id)? {
   662→            Some(i) => i,
   663→            None => return Ok(None),
   664→        };
   665→
   666→        let mut stmt = self
   667→            .conn
   668→            .prepare(
   669→                "SELECT id, issue_id, status, phase_count, current_phase, iteration, summary, error, branch_name, pr_url, team_id, has_team, started_at, completed_at
   670→                 FROM pipeline_runs WHERE issue_id = ?1 ORDER BY id",
   671→            )
   672→            .context("Failed to prepare get_issue_detail runs")?;
   673→        let rows = stmt
   674→            .query_map(params![id], |row| {
   675→                Ok(PipelineRunRow {
   676→                    id: row.get(0)?,
   677→                    issue_id: row.get(1)?,
   678→                    status: row.get(2)?,
   679→                    phase_count: row.get(3)?,
   680→                    current_phase: row.get(4)?,
   681→                    iteration: row.get(5)?,
   682→                    summary: row.get(6)?,
   683→                    error: row.get(7)?,
   684→                    branch_name: row.get(8)?,
   685→                    pr_url: row.get(9)?,
   686→                    team_id: row.get(10)?,
   687→                    has_team: row.get(11)?,
   688→                    started_at: row.get(12)?,
   689→                    completed_at: row.get(13)?,
   690→                })
   691→            })
   692→            .context("Failed to query pipeline runs")?;
   693→        let mut runs = Vec::new();
   694→        for row in rows {
   695→            let r = row.context("Failed to read pipeline_run row")?;
   696→            let run = r.into_pipeline_run()?;
   697→            let phases = self.get_pipeline_phases(run.id)?;
   698→            runs.push(PipelineRunDetail { run, phases });
   699→        }
   700→
   701→        Ok(Some(IssueDetail { issue, runs }))
   702→    }
   703→
   704→    // ── Pipeline runs ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

     5→/// A project registered in the Factory, representing a local codebase that can
     6→/// have issues managed on the Kanban board and executed by the agent pipeline.
     7→///
     8→/// Projects are created via the Factory API and stored in SQLite. Each project
     9→/// has an associated filesystem path that the pipeline uses when checking out
    10→/// worktrees and running `forge` commands.
    11→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
    12→pub struct Project {
    13→    /// Unique integer identifier assigned by the database.
    14→    pub id: i64,
    15→    /// Human-readable name shown in the Factory UI.

    17→    /// Absolute filesystem path to the project root on the host machine.
    18→    pub path: String,
    19→    /// Optional GitHub repository slug (`owner/repo`) used for auto-PR creation.
    20→    pub github_repo: Option<String>,
    21→    /// ISO 8601 timestamp of when the project was registered.
    22→    pub created_at: String,

    24→
    25→/// The column an issue currently occupies in the Kanban board.
    26→///
    27→/// Columns define the workflow lifecycle of an issue. Moving an issue to
    28→/// `InProgress` triggers automatic pipeline execution via [`crate::factory::pipeline::PipelineRunner`].
    29→/// The typical progression is:
    30→///
    31→/// `Backlog` → `Ready` → `InProgress` → `InReview` → `Done`
    32→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
    33→#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
    34→pub enum IssueColumn {
    35→    /// Work that has been captured but is not yet scheduled for development.
    36→    Backlog,
    37→    /// Scheduled and groomed work that is ready to be picked up by the pipeline.
    38→    Ready,
    39→    /// Issue is currently being implemented by the agent pipeline.
    40→    ///
    41→    /// Moving into this column triggers a [`PipelineRun`] to be created and
    42→    /// execution to begin via [`crate::factory::pipeline::PipelineRunner`].
    43→    InProgress,
    44→    /// Implementation is complete and awaiting human or automated review.
    45→    InReview,
    46→    /// Work is accepted and complete. Terminal state.
    47→    Done,
    48→}
    49→
    50→impl IssueColumn {
    51→    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
    52→        match self {
    53→            Self::Backlog => "backlog",
    54→            Self::Ready => "ready",
    55→            Self::InProgress => "in_progress",
    56→            Self::InReview => "in_review",
    57→            Self::Done => "done",
    58→        }
    59→    }
    60→}
    61→
    62→impl FromStr for IssueColumn {
    63→    type Err = String;
