    65→    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
    66→        match s {
    67→            "backlog" => Ok(Self::Backlog),
    68→            "ready" => Ok(Self::Ready),
    69→            "in_progress" => Ok(Self::InProgress),
    70→            "in_review" => Ok(Self::InReview),
    71→            "done" => Ok(Self::Done),
    72→            _ => Err(format!("Invalid column: {}", s)),
    73→        }
    74→    }
    75→}

   120→/// A Kanban issue representing a unit of work to be implemented by the agent pipeline.
   121→///
   122→/// Issues are the primary work items in the Factory. They are displayed on the
   123→/// Kanban board and move through columns as they progress. When an issue is moved
   124→/// to [`IssueColumn::InProgress`], the Factory API triggers a [`PipelineRun`] that
   125→/// invokes `forge` to implement the issue autonomously.
   126→///
   127→/// Issues can optionally be linked to a GitHub issue via `github_issue_number`.
   128→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   129→pub struct Issue {
   130→    /// Unique integer identifier assigned by the database.
   131→    pub id: i64,
   132→    /// Foreign key to the [`Project`] this issue belongs to.
   133→    pub project_id: i64,
   134→    /// Short summary of the work, shown as the card title on the board.
   135→    pub title: String,
   136→    /// Full specification of the work, passed as the prompt to the pipeline.
   137→    pub description: String,
   138→    /// Current Kanban column the issue occupies.
   139→    pub column: IssueColumn,
   140→    /// Sort order within the column. Lower values appear higher on the board.
   141→    pub position: i32,
   142→    /// Urgency level used for triage and display ordering.
   143→    pub priority: Priority,
   144→    /// Arbitrary string tags for filtering and categorisation (e.g. `["bug", "ui"]`).
   145→    pub labels: Vec<String>,
   146→    /// GitHub issue number if this card was synced from or linked to a GitHub issue.
   147→    pub github_issue_number: Option<i64>,
   148→    /// ISO 8601 timestamp of when the issue was created.
   149→    pub created_at: String,
   150→    /// ISO 8601 timestamp of the most recent update to this issue.
   151→    pub updated_at: String,
   152→}
   153→
   154→/// State machine for a [`PipelineRun`].
   155→///
   156→/// Transitions follow the path: `Queued` → `Running` → `Completed` | `Failed` | `Cancelled`.
   157→/// Terminal states are identified by [`PipelineStatus::is_terminal`].
   158→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
   159→#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
   160→pub enum PipelineStatus {
   161→    /// The run has been created and is waiting for an executor slot.
   162→    Queued,
   163→    /// The pipeline is actively executing phases via `forge`.
   164→    Running,
   165→    /// All phases finished successfully. Terminal state.
   166→    Completed,
   167→    /// The pipeline encountered an unrecoverable error. Terminal state.
   168→    Failed,
   169→    /// The run was explicitly cancelled before completion. Terminal state.
   170→    Cancelled,
   171→}
   172→
   173→impl PipelineStatus {
   174→    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
   175→        match self {
   176→            Self::Queued => "queued",
   177→            Self::Running => "running",
   178→            Self::Completed => "completed",
   179→            Self::Failed => "failed",
   180→            Self::Cancelled => "cancelled",
   181→        }
   182→    }
   183→}
   184→
   185→impl FromStr for PipelineStatus {
   186→    type Err = String;
   187→
   188→    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
   189→        match s {
   190→            "queued" => Ok(Self::Queued),
   191→            "running" => Ok(Self::Running),
   192→            "completed" => Ok(Self::Completed),
   193→            "failed" => Ok(Self::Failed),
   194→            "cancelled" => Ok(Self::Cancelled),
   195→            _ => Err(format!("Invalid pipeline status: {}", s)),
   196→        }
   197→    }
   198→}
   199→
   200→impl PipelineStatus {
   201→    /// Returns true for terminal states that indicate the pipeline is no longer running.
   202→    pub fn is_terminal(&self) -> bool {
   203→        matches!(self, Self::Completed | Self::Failed | Self::Cancelled)
   204→    }
   205→}
   206→
   207→/// A single execution of the Forge pipeline triggered by an issue moving to `InProgress`.
   208→///
   209→/// Each `PipelineRun` corresponds to one invocation of `forge` against a specific
   210→/// [`Issue`]. The run tracks progress through phases, the current iteration within
   211→/// a phase, and the outcome (summary, error, PR URL). When agent-team execution is
   212→/// enabled, the run is associated with an [`AgentTeam`] via `team_id`.
   213→///
   214→/// Runs are persisted in SQLite and their state is broadcast to connected WebSocket
   215→/// clients so the UI can display live progress.
   216→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   217→pub struct PipelineRun {
   218→    /// Unique integer identifier assigned by the database.
   219→    pub id: i64,
   220→    /// Foreign key to the [`Issue`] that triggered this run.
   221→    pub issue_id: i64,
   222→    /// Current lifecycle state of the run.
   223→    pub status: PipelineStatus,
   224→    /// Total number of phases in the spec being executed, once known.
   225→    pub phase_count: Option<i32>,
   226→    /// Index of the phase currently being executed (1-based).
   227→    pub current_phase: Option<i32>,
   228→    /// Current iteration number within the active phase.
   229→    pub iteration: Option<i32>,
   230→    /// Human-readable summary produced at the end of a successful run.
   231→    pub summary: Option<String>,
   232→    /// Error message captured when the run transitions to `Failed`.
   233→    pub error: Option<String>,
   234→    /// Git branch created for this run (e.g. `forge/issue-42`).
   235→    pub branch_name: Option<String>,
   236→    /// URL of the pull request opened after a successful run, if any.
   237→    pub pr_url: Option<String>,
   238→    /// Foreign key to the [`AgentTeam`] coordinating this run, if agent-team mode is active.
   239→    #[serde(default)]
   240→    pub team_id: Option<i64>,
   241→    /// `true` when this run is being executed by an agent team rather than a single pipeline.
   242→    #[serde(default)]
   243→    pub has_team: bool,
   244→    /// ISO 8601 timestamp of when execution began.
   245→    pub started_at: String,
   246→    /// ISO 8601 timestamp of when the run reached a terminal state, if applicable.
   247→    pub completed_at: Option<String>,
   248→}
   249→
   250→/// Status of a pipeline phase.
   251→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   252→#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
   253→pub enum PhaseStatus {
   254→    /// Phase has not started yet.
   255→    Pending,
   256→    /// Phase is currently executing iterations.
   257→    Running,
   258→    /// Phase finished successfully (agent emitted `<promise>DONE</promise>`).
   259→    Completed,
   260→    /// Phase exceeded its iteration budget or encountered an error.
   261→    Failed,
   262→}
   263→
   264→impl std::fmt::Display for PhaseStatus {
   265→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
   266→        match self {
   267→            Self::Pending => write!(f, "pending"),
   268→            Self::Running => write!(f, "running"),
   269→            Self::Completed => write!(f, "completed"),
   270→            Self::Failed => write!(f, "failed"),
   271→        }
   272→    }
   273→}
   274→
   275→impl std::str::FromStr for PhaseStatus {
   276→    type Err = anyhow::Error;
   277→    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
   278→        match s {
   279→            "pending" => Ok(Self::Pending),
   280→            "running" => Ok(Self::Running),
   281→            "completed" => Ok(Self::Completed),
   282→            "failed" => Ok(Self::Failed),
   283→            other => anyhow::bail!("Invalid phase status: {}", other),
   284→        }
   285→    }
   286→}

   288→/// A single named phase within a [`PipelineRun`], tracking iteration progress.
   289→///
   290→/// Phases map directly to the phases defined in the Forge spec file (`.forge/spec.md`).
   291→/// Each phase has an iteration budget; the pipeline retries the phase until the agent
   292→/// emits `<promise>DONE</promise>` or the budget is exhausted.
   293→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   294→pub struct PipelinePhase {

   305→    /// Current iteration count within this phase (incremented on each retry).

   308→    pub budget: Option<i32>,

   313→    /// Error message if the phase failed.

   315→}

   378→// Agent team execution models
   379→
   380→/// Isolation strategy for agent task execution.
   381→///
   382→/// Controls how each [`AgentTask`] within a team is isolated from others to
   383→/// prevent file-system conflicts when multiple agents work in parallel.
   384→///
   385→/// Currently only `Worktree` and `Shared` are fully implemented.
   386→/// `Container` and `Hybrid` are reserved for future use.
   387→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   388→#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
   389→pub enum IsolationStrategy {
   390→    /// Each agent task operates in a dedicated `git worktree` branched from `main`.
   391→    /// Results are merged back after each wave completes.
   392→    Worktree,
   393→    /// Each agent task runs in an isolated Docker container. Reserved for future use.
   394→    Container,
   395→    /// Combines container and worktree isolation. Reserved for future use.
   396→    Hybrid,
   397→    /// All agent tasks share the same working directory. Suitable for sequential strategies
   398→    /// or when tasks are guaranteed to touch non-overlapping files.
   399→    Shared,
   400→}
   401→
   402→impl IsolationStrategy {
   403→    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
   404→        match self {
   405→            Self::Worktree => "worktree",
   406→            Self::Container => "container",
   407→            Self::Hybrid => "hybrid",
   408→            Self::Shared => "shared",
   409→        }
   410→    }
   411→}
   412→
   413→impl std::fmt::Display for IsolationStrategy {
   414→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
   415→        f.write_str(self.as_str())
   416→    }
   417→}
   418→
   419→impl FromStr for IsolationStrategy {
   420→    type Err = String;
   421→
   422→    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
   423→        match s {
   424→            "worktree" => Ok(Self::Worktree),
   425→            "container" => Ok(Self::Container),
   426→            "hybrid" => Ok(Self::Hybrid),
   427→            "shared" => Ok(Self::Shared),
   428→            _ => Err(format!("Invalid isolation strategy: {}", s)),
   429→        }
   430→    }
   431→}
   432→
   433→/// Roles that agents can assume during team execution of a pipeline run.
   434→///
   435→/// The planner assigns `Coder`, `Tester`, and `Reviewer` tasks when decomposing
   436→/// an issue. `Planner`, `BrowserVerifier`, and `TestVerifier` are intended for
   437→/// future system-assigned specialisation and are not yet fully utilised.
   438→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   439→#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
   440→pub enum AgentRole {
   441→    /// Orchestrates the team by producing the execution plan. System-assigned.
   442→    Planner,
   443→    /// Writes and modifies source code to implement the issue requirements.
   444→    Coder,
   445→    /// Writes and runs automated tests to verify the implementation.
   446→    Tester,
   447→    /// Reviews code changes for correctness, style, and adherence to requirements.
   448→    Reviewer,
   449→    /// Verifies the implementation via browser automation. Reserved for future use.
   450→    BrowserVerifier,
   451→    /// Verifies the implementation by running the test suite. Reserved for future use.
   452→    TestVerifier,
   453→}
   454→
   455→impl AgentRole {
   456→    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
   457→        match self {
   458→            Self::Planner => "planner",
   459→            Self::Coder => "coder",
   460→            Self::Tester => "tester",
   461→            Self::Reviewer => "reviewer",
   462→            Self::BrowserVerifier => "browser_verifier",
   463→            Self::TestVerifier => "test_verifier",
   464→        }
   465→    }
   466→}
   467→
   468→impl std::fmt::Display for AgentRole {
   469→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
   470→        f.write_str(self.as_str())
   471→    }
   472→}
   473→
   474→impl FromStr for AgentRole {
   475→    type Err = String;
   476→
   477→    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
   478→        match s {
   479→            "planner" => Ok(Self::Planner),
   480→            "coder" => Ok(Self::Coder),
   481→            "tester" => Ok(Self::Tester),
   482→            "reviewer" => Ok(Self::Reviewer),
   483→            "browser_verifier" => Ok(Self::BrowserVerifier),
   484→            "test_verifier" => Ok(Self::TestVerifier),
   485→            _ => Err(format!("Invalid agent role: {}", s)),
   486→        }
   487→    }
   488→}
   489→
   490→/// Lifecycle state of an individual [`AgentTask`].
   491→///
   492→/// Transitions follow the path: `Pending` → `Running` → `Completed` | `Failed` | `Cancelled`.
   493→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   494→#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
   495→pub enum AgentTaskStatus {
   496→    /// Task has been created but its wave has not started executing yet.
   497→    Pending,
   498→    /// Task is actively running its assigned agent process.
   499→    Running,
   500→    /// Task finished successfully. Terminal state.
   501→    Completed,
   502→    /// Task encountered an unrecoverable error. Terminal state.
   503→    Failed,
   504→    /// Task was cancelled, typically because a sibling task in the same wave failed. Terminal state.
   505→    Cancelled,
   506→}
   507→
   508→impl AgentTaskStatus {
   509→    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
   510→        match self {
   511→            Self::Pending => "pending",
   512→            Self::Running => "running",
   513→            Self::Completed => "completed",
   514→            Self::Failed => "failed",
   515→            Self::Cancelled => "cancelled",
   516→        }
   517→    }
   518→}
   519→
   520→impl std::fmt::Display for AgentTaskStatus {
   521→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
   522→        f.write_str(self.as_str())
   523→    }
   524→}
   525→
   526→impl FromStr for AgentTaskStatus {
   527→    type Err = String;
   528→
   529→    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
   530→        match s {
   531→            "pending" => Ok(Self::Pending),
   532→            "running" => Ok(Self::Running),
   533→            "completed" => Ok(Self::Completed),
   534→            "failed" => Ok(Self::Failed),
   535→            "cancelled" => Ok(Self::Cancelled),
   536→            _ => Err(format!("Invalid agent task status: {}", s)),
   537→        }
   538→    }
   539→}
   540→
   541→/// Types of events emitted by agents during task execution.
   542→///
   543→/// Events are persisted as [`AgentEvent`] records and streamed to the UI via
   544→/// WebSocket to provide live visibility into what each agent is doing.
   545→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   546→#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
   547→pub enum AgentEventType {
   548→    /// Internal reasoning or planning text from the agent (may be verbose).
   549→    Thinking,
   550→    /// A concrete action the agent is taking (e.g. editing a file, running a command).
   551→    Action,
   552→    /// Substantive output produced by the agent (e.g. code, test results).
   553→    Output,
   554→    /// A structured signal emitted via `<progress>`, `<blocker>`, or `<pivot>` tags.
   555→    Signal,
   556→    /// An error encountered by the agent during task execution.
   557→    Error,
   558→}
   559→
   560→impl AgentEventType {
   561→    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
   562→        match self {
   563→            Self::Thinking => "thinking",
   564→            Self::Action => "action",
   565→            Self::Output => "output",
   566→            Self::Signal => "signal",
   567→            Self::Error => "error",
   568→        }
   569→    }
   570→}
   571→
   572→impl std::fmt::Display for AgentEventType {
   573→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
   574→        f.write_str(self.as_str())
   575→    }
   576→}
   577→
   578→impl FromStr for AgentEventType {
   579→    type Err = String;
   580→
   581→    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
   582→        match s {
   583→            "thinking" => Ok(Self::Thinking),
   584→            "action" => Ok(Self::Action),
   585→            "output" => Ok(Self::Output),
   586→            "signal" => Ok(Self::Signal),
   587→            "error" => Ok(Self::Error),
   588→            _ => Err(format!("Invalid agent event type: {}", s)),
   589→        }
   590→    }
   591→}
   592→
   593→/// How the tasks within an [`AgentTeam`] are scheduled relative to each other.
   594→///
   595→/// The strategy is chosen by the planner based on the nature of the issue and
   596→/// stored on the [`AgentTeam`]. It determines how the executor launches tasks
   597→/// and whether inter-task dependencies are enforced.
   598→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   599→#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
   600→pub enum ExecutionStrategy {
   601→    /// All tasks are started simultaneously with no ordering constraints.
   602→    Parallel,
   603→    /// Tasks are executed one at a time in dependency order.
   604→    Sequential,
   605→    /// Tasks are executed in waves: all tasks in wave N complete before wave N+1 begins.
   606→    /// This is the primary strategy for Coder → Tester → Reviewer pipelines.
   607→    WavePipeline,
   608→    /// The executor dynamically adjusts scheduling based on runtime signals. Reserved for future use.
   609→    Adaptive,
   610→}
   611→
   612→impl ExecutionStrategy {
   613→    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
   614→        match self {
   615→            Self::Parallel => "parallel",
   616→            Self::Sequential => "sequential",
   617→            Self::WavePipeline => "wave_pipeline",
   618→            Self::Adaptive => "adaptive",
   619→        }
   620→    }
   621→}
   622→
   623→impl std::fmt::Display for ExecutionStrategy {
   624→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
   625→        f.write_str(self.as_str())
   626→    }
   627→}
   628→
   629→impl FromStr for ExecutionStrategy {
   630→    type Err = String;
   631→
   632→    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
   633→        match s {
   634→            "parallel" => Ok(Self::Parallel),
   635→            "sequential" => Ok(Self::Sequential),
   636→            "wave_pipeline" => Ok(Self::WavePipeline),
   637→            "adaptive" => Ok(Self::Adaptive),
   638→            _ => Err(format!("Unknown execution strategy: {}", s)),
   639→        }
   640→    }
   641→}
   642→
   643→/// Type of structured signal emitted by an agent inside `<signal>` XML tags.
   644→///
   645→/// Signals are parsed from agent output and stored as [`AgentEvent`] records
   646→/// with `event_type = Signal`. They correspond to the `<progress>`, `<blocker>`,
   647→/// and `<pivot>` tags described in the Forge spec.
   648→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   649→#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
   650→pub enum SignalType {
   651→    /// Agent is making forward progress. Resets the stall detector.
   652→    Progress,
   653→    /// Agent has hit an obstacle that prevents it from continuing without intervention.
   654→    Blocker,
   655→    /// Agent is changing its approach due to new information or a dead end.
   656→    Pivot,
   657→}
   658→
   659→impl SignalType {
   660→    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
   661→        match self {
   662→            Self::Progress => "progress",
   663→            Self::Blocker => "blocker",
   664→            Self::Pivot => "pivot",
   665→        }
   666→    }
   667→}
   668→
   669→impl std::fmt::Display for SignalType {
   670→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
   671→        f.write_str(self.as_str())
   672→    }
   673→}
   674→
   675→impl FromStr for SignalType {
   676→    type Err = String;
   677→    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
   678→        match s {
   679→            "progress" => Ok(Self::Progress),
   680→            "blocker" => Ok(Self::Blocker),
   681→            "pivot" => Ok(Self::Pivot),
   682→            _ => Err(format!("Unknown signal type: {}", s)),
   683→        }
   684→    }
   685→}

   687→/// The method used to verify that a pipeline run's output is correct.
   688→///
   689→/// Verification tasks are assigned to `BrowserVerifier` or `TestVerifier` agents
   690→/// and run after the primary implementation wave completes.
   691→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   692→#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
   693→pub enum VerificationType {
   694→    /// Verification is performed by navigating the application in a headless browser.
   695→    Browser,
   696→    /// Verification is performed by building the project and running its test suite.
   697→    TestBuild,
   698→}
   699→
   700→impl VerificationType {
   701→    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
   702→        match self {
   703→            Self::Browser => "browser",
   704→            Self::TestBuild => "test_build",
   705→        }
   706→    }
   707→}
   708→
   709→impl std::fmt::Display for VerificationType {
   710→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
   711→        f.write_str(self.as_str())
   712→    }
   713→}
   714→
   715→impl FromStr for VerificationType {
   716→    type Err = String;
   717→    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
   718→        match s {
   719→            "browser" => Ok(Self::Browser),
   720→            "test_build" => Ok(Self::TestBuild),
   721→            _ => Err(format!("Unknown verification type: {}", s)),
   722→        }
   723→    }

   725→
   726→/// A team of agents assembled to execute a single [`PipelineRun`].
   727→///
   728→/// Created by the planner after it analyses the issue and decomposes it into
   729→/// discrete tasks. The team record captures the high-level plan and the chosen
   730→/// execution and isolation strategies. Individual work items are stored as
   731→/// [`AgentTask`] records linked to this team.
   732→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   733→pub struct AgentTeam {
   734→    /// Unique integer identifier assigned by the database.
   735→    pub id: i64,
   736→    /// Foreign key to the [`PipelineRun`] this team is executing.
   737→    pub run_id: i64,
   738→    /// How tasks within the team are scheduled relative to each other.
   739→    pub strategy: ExecutionStrategy,
   740→    /// How each task is isolated from the others on the filesystem.
   741→    pub isolation: IsolationStrategy,
   742→    /// Human-readable summary of the plan produced by the planner agent.
   743→    pub plan_summary: String,
   744→    /// ISO 8601 timestamp of when the team was created.
   745→    pub created_at: String,
   746→}
   747→
   748→/// An individual task within an [`AgentTeam`], assigned to a specific agent role.
   749→///
   750→/// Tasks represent atomic units of work (e.g. "implement the login handler",
   751→/// "write tests for the login handler"). They are organized into numbered waves
   752→/// so that dependency ordering can be enforced: all tasks in wave N complete
   753→/// before wave N+1 starts.
   754→///
   755→/// Depending on the [`IsolationStrategy`], each task may run in its own `git worktree`
   756→/// (tracked by `worktree_path`) or a container (tracked by `container_id`).
   757→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   758→pub struct AgentTask {
   759→    /// Unique integer identifier assigned by the database.
   760→    pub id: i64,
   761→    /// Foreign key to the [`AgentTeam`] this task belongs to.
   762→    pub team_id: i64,
   763→    /// Short name describing what this specific task does (e.g. `"Implement auth middleware"`).
   764→    pub name: String,
   765→    /// Full instructions passed to the agent as its prompt for this task.
   766→    pub description: String,
   767→    /// The role played by the agent executing this task.
   768→    pub agent_role: AgentRole,
   769→    /// Wave number for dependency-ordered execution. Tasks in the same wave can run in parallel.
   770→    pub wave: i32,
   771→    /// IDs of other [`AgentTask`]s that must complete before this task can start.
   772→    pub depends_on: Vec<i64>,
   773→    /// Current lifecycle state of this task.
   774→    pub status: AgentTaskStatus,
   775→    /// Isolation mode used for this task's filesystem environment.
   776→    pub isolation_type: IsolationStrategy,
   777→    /// Filesystem path to the git worktree created for this task, if using worktree isolation.
   778→    pub worktree_path: Option<String>,
   779→    /// Docker container ID for this task, if using container isolation.
   780→    pub container_id: Option<String>,
   781→    /// Git branch name used by this task (typically `forge/issue-<id>-<role>`).
   782→    pub branch_name: Option<String>,
   783→    /// ISO 8601 timestamp of when this task began executing.
   784→    pub started_at: Option<String>,
   785→    /// ISO 8601 timestamp of when this task reached a terminal state.
   786→    pub completed_at: Option<String>,
   787→    /// Error message captured when the task transitions to `Failed`.
   788→    pub error: Option<String>,
   789→}
   790→
   791→/// A real-time event emitted by an agent during task execution.
   792→///
   793→/// Events are written to the database as they occur and broadcast to connected
   794→/// WebSocket clients so the Factory UI can display a live log of what each agent
   795→/// is doing. The `metadata` field carries structured data for `Signal` events
   796→/// (e.g. the parsed `<progress>` or `<blocker>` payload).
   797→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   798→pub struct AgentEvent {
   799→    /// Unique integer identifier assigned by the database.
   800→    pub id: i64,
   801→    /// Foreign key to the [`AgentTask`] that emitted this event.
   802→    pub task_id: i64,
   803→    /// Classifies the nature of the event for filtering and display.
   804→    pub event_type: AgentEventType,
   805→    /// The raw textual content of the event.
   806→    pub content: String,
   807→    /// Optional structured JSON payload, present for `Signal` events.
   808→    pub metadata: Option<serde_json::Value>,
   809→    /// ISO 8601 timestamp of when the event was recorded.
   810→    pub created_at: String,
   811→}
   812→
   813→/// Aggregated view of an [`AgentTeam`] together with all of its tasks.

   816→/// dashboard panel in the Factory UI to render the full team status at a glance.
   817→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   818→pub struct AgentTeamDetail {
   819→    /// The agent team metadata and configuration.
   820→    pub team: AgentTeam,
   821→    /// All tasks belonging to the team, ordered by wave then task ID.
   822→    pub tasks: Vec<AgentTask>,
   823→}

   706→    pub fn create_pipeline_run(&self, issue_id: i64) -> Result<PipelineRun> {
   707→        self.conn
   708→            .execute(
   709→                "INSERT INTO pipeline_runs (issue_id) VALUES (?1)",
   710→                params![issue_id],
   711→            )
   712→            .context("Failed to insert pipeline run")?;
   713→        let id = self.conn.last_insert_rowid();
   714→        self.get_pipeline_run(id)?
   715→            .context("Pipeline run not found after insert")
   716→    }
   717→
   718→    pub fn update_pipeline_run(
   719→        &self,
   720→        id: i64,
   721→        status: &PipelineStatus,
   722→        summary: Option<&str>,
   723→        error: Option<&str>,
   724→    ) -> Result<PipelineRun> {
   725→        let is_terminal = status.is_terminal();
   726→
   727→        if is_terminal {
   728→            self.conn
   729→                .execute(
   730→                    "UPDATE pipeline_runs SET status = ?1, summary = ?2, error = ?3, completed_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?4",
   731→                    params![status.as_str(), summary, error, id],
   732→                )
   733→                .context("Failed to update pipeline run")?;
   734→        } else {
   735→            self.conn
   736→                .execute(
   737→                    "UPDATE pipeline_runs SET status = ?1, summary = ?2, error = ?3 WHERE id = ?4",
   738→                    params![status.as_str(), summary, error, id],
   739→                )
   740→                .context("Failed to update pipeline run")?;
   741→        }
   742→
   743→        self.get_pipeline_run(id)?
   744→            .context("Pipeline run not found after update")
   745→    }
   746→
   747→    pub fn get_pipeline_run(&self, id: i64) -> Result<Option<PipelineRun>> {
   748→        let mut stmt = self
   749→            .conn
   750→            .prepare(
   751→                "SELECT id, issue_id, status, phase_count, current_phase, iteration, summary, error, branch_name, pr_url, team_id, has_team, started_at, completed_at
   752→                 FROM pipeline_runs WHERE id = ?1",
   753→            )
   754→            .context("Failed to prepare get_pipeline_run")?;
   755→        let mut rows = stmt
   756→            .query_map(params![id], |row| {
   757→                Ok(PipelineRunRow {
   758→                    id: row.get(0)?,
   759→                    issue_id: row.get(1)?,
   760→                    status: row.get(2)?,
   761→                    phase_count: row.get(3)?,
   762→                    current_phase: row.get(4)?,
   763→                    iteration: row.get(5)?,
   764→                    summary: row.get(6)?,
   765→                    error: row.get(7)?,
   766→                    branch_name: row.get(8)?,
   767→                    pr_url: row.get(9)?,
   768→                    team_id: row.get(10)?,
   769→                    has_team: row.get(11)?,
   770→                    started_at: row.get(12)?,
   771→                    completed_at: row.get(13)?,
   772→                })
   773→            })
   774→            .context("Failed to query pipeline run")?;
   775→        match rows.next() {
   776→            Some(row) => {
   777→                let r = row.context("Failed to read pipeline_run row")?;
   778→                Ok(Some(r.into_pipeline_run()?))
   779→            }
   780→            None => Ok(None),
   781→        }
   782→    }
   783→
   784→    pub fn cancel_pipeline_run(&self, id: i64) -> Result<PipelineRun> {
   785→        self.update_pipeline_run(id, &PipelineStatus::Cancelled, None, None)
   786→    }
   787→
   788→    /// Mark all orphaned "running" or "queued" pipeline runs as failed.
   789→    /// Called on server startup to clean up runs from a previous server instance
   790→    /// whose subprocesses no longer exist.
   791→    pub fn recover_orphaned_runs(&self) -> Result<usize> {
   792→        let count = self.conn.execute(
   793→            "UPDATE pipeline_runs SET status = 'failed', error = 'Server restarted — pipeline process was lost', completed_at = datetime('now') WHERE status IN ('running', 'queued')",
   794→            [],
   795→        ).context("Failed to recover orphaned pipeline runs")?;
   796→
   797→        // Also move any in_progress issues (whose runs are now failed) back to ready
   798→        if count > 0 {
   799→            self.conn.execute(
   800→                "UPDATE issues SET column_name = 'ready' WHERE column_name = 'in_progress' AND id IN (SELECT issue_id FROM pipeline_runs WHERE error = 'Server restarted — pipeline process was lost')",
   801→                [],
   802→            ).context("Failed to move orphaned issues back to ready")?;
   803→        }
   804→
   805→        Ok(count)
   806→    }
   807→

   821→            .context("Failed to update pipeline progress")?;

   849→
   850→    // ── Pipeline Phase CRUD ─────────────────────────────────────────
   851→
   852→    /// Create or update a pipeline phase record.
   853→    ///
   854→    /// Accepts a `&str` status for backward compatibility with existing callers.
   855→    /// The status string is stored as-is in the database and parsed into
   856→    /// `PhaseStatus` when reading back via `get_pipeline_phases`.
   857→    pub fn upsert_pipeline_phase(
   858→        &self,
   859→        run_id: i64,
   860→        phase_number: &str,
   861→        phase_name: &str,
   862→        status: &str,
   863→        iteration: Option<i32>,
   864→        budget: Option<i32>,
   865→    ) -> Result<()> {
   866→        self.conn
   867→            .execute(
   868→                "INSERT INTO pipeline_phases (run_id, phase_number, phase_name, status, iteration, budget, started_at)
   869→                 VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, datetime('now'))
   870→                 ON CONFLICT(run_id, phase_number) DO UPDATE SET
   871→                    status = ?4,
   872→                    iteration = COALESCE(?5, pipeline_phases.iteration),
   873→                    budget = COALESCE(?6, pipeline_phases.budget),
   874→                    completed_at = CASE WHEN ?4 IN ('completed', 'failed') THEN datetime('now') ELSE pipeline_phases.completed_at END,
   875→                    error = NULL",
   876→                params![run_id, phase_number, phase_name, status, iteration, budget],
   877→            )
   878→            .context("Failed to upsert pipeline phase")?;
   879→        Ok(())
   880→    }
   881→
   882→    /// Create or update a pipeline phase record using a typed `PhaseStatus`.
   883→    pub fn upsert_pipeline_phase_typed(
   884→        &self,
   885→        run_id: i64,
   886→        phase_number: &str,
   887→        phase_name: &str,
   888→        status: &PhaseStatus,
   889→        iteration: Option<i32>,
   890→        budget: Option<i32>,
   891→    ) -> Result<()> {
   892→        self.upsert_pipeline_phase(
   893→            run_id,
   894→            phase_number,
   895→            phase_name,
   896→            &status.to_string(),
   897→            iteration,
   898→            budget,
   899→        )
   900→    }
   901→
   902→    /// Get all phases for a pipeline run.
   903→    pub fn get_pipeline_phases(&self, run_id: i64) -> Result<Vec<PipelinePhase>> {
   904→        let mut stmt = self
   905→            .conn
   906→            .prepare(
   907→                "SELECT id, run_id, phase_number, phase_name, status, iteration, budget, started_at, completed_at, error
   908→                 FROM pipeline_phases WHERE run_id = ?1 ORDER BY phase_number ASC",
   909→            )
   910→            .context("Failed to prepare get_pipeline_phases")?;
   911→        let rows = stmt
   912→            .query_map(params![run_id], |row| {
   913→                let status_str: String = row.get(4)?;
   914→                let status = status_str
   915→                    .parse::<PhaseStatus>()
   916→                    .unwrap_or(PhaseStatus::Pending);
   917→                Ok(PipelinePhase {
   918→                    id: row.get(0)?,
   919→                    run_id: row.get(1)?,
   920→                    phase_number: row.get(2)?,
   921→                    phase_name: row.get(3)?,
   922→                    status,
   923→                    iteration: row.get(5)?,
   924→                    budget: row.get(6)?,
   925→                    started_at: row.get(7)?,
   926→                    completed_at: row.get(8)?,
   927→                    error: row.get(9)?,
   928→                })
   929→            })
   930→            .context("Failed to query pipeline phases")?;
   931→
   932→        let mut phases = Vec::new();
   933→        for row in rows {
   934→            phases.push(row.context("Failed to read pipeline phase row")?);
   935→        }
   936→        Ok(phases)
   937→    }

  1033→    // ── Agent Tasks ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

  1035→    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
  1036→    pub fn create_agent_task(
  1037→        &self,
  1038→        team_id: i64,
  1039→        name: &str,
  1040→        description: &str,
  1041→        agent_role: &AgentRole,
  1042→        wave: i32,
  1043→        depends_on: &[i64],
  1044→        isolation_type: &IsolationStrategy,
  1045→    ) -> Result<AgentTask> {
  1046→        let depends_json =
  1047→            serde_json::to_string(depends_on).context("Failed to serialize depends_on")?;
  1048→        self.conn.execute(
  1049→            "INSERT INTO agent_tasks (team_id, name, description, agent_role, wave, depends_on, isolation_type) \
  1050→             VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, ?7)",
  1051→            params![team_id, name, description, agent_role.as_str(), wave, depends_json, isolation_type.as_str()],
  1052→        ).context("Failed to insert agent task")?;
  1053→        let id = self.conn.last_insert_rowid();
  1054→        Ok(AgentTask {
  1055→            id,
  1056→            team_id,
  1057→            name: name.to_string(),
  1058→            description: description.to_string(),
  1059→            agent_role: agent_role.clone(),
  1060→            wave,
  1061→            depends_on: depends_on.to_vec(),
  1062→            status: AgentTaskStatus::Pending,
  1063→            isolation_type: isolation_type.clone(),
  1064→            worktree_path: None,
  1065→            container_id: None,
  1066→            branch_name: None,
  1067→            started_at: None,
  1068→            completed_at: None,
  1069→            error: None,
  1070→        })
  1071→    }

  1073→    pub fn update_agent_task_status(
  1074→        &self,
  1075→        task_id: i64,
  1076→        status: &AgentTaskStatus,
  1077→        error: Option<&str>,
  1078→    ) -> Result<()> {
  1079→        let status_str = status.as_str();
  1080→        match status {
  1081→            AgentTaskStatus::Running => {

  1256→    pub fn create_agent_event(
  1257→        &self,
  1258→        task_id: i64,
  1259→        event_type: &str,
  1260→        content: &str,
  1261→        metadata: Option<&serde_json::Value>,
  1262→    ) -> Result<AgentEvent> {
  1263→        let metadata_str = match metadata {
  1264→            Some(m) => {
  1265→                Some(serde_json::to_string(m).context("Failed to serialize event metadata")?)
  1266→            }
  1267→            None => None,
  1268→        };
  1269→        self.conn.execute(
  1270→            "INSERT INTO agent_events (task_id, event_type, content, metadata) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)",
  1271→            params![task_id, event_type, content, metadata_str],
  1272→        ).context("Failed to insert agent event")?;
  1273→        let id = self.conn.last_insert_rowid();
  1274→        Ok(AgentEvent {
  1275→            id,
  1276→            task_id,
  1277→            event_type: {
  1278→                let val = event_type;
  1279→                val.parse()
  1280→                    .map_err(|_| anyhow::anyhow!("invalid event_type in database: '{}'", val))?
  1281→            },
  1282→            content: content.to_string(),
  1283→            metadata: metadata.cloned(),
  1284→            created_at: chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339(),
  1285→        })
  1286→    }
  1287→
  1288→    pub fn get_agent_events(
  1289→        &self,
  1290→        task_id: i64,

  1292→        offset: i64,
  1293→    ) -> Result<Vec<AgentEvent>> {
  1294→        let mut stmt = self
  1295→            .conn
  1296→            .prepare(
  1297→                "SELECT id, task_id, event_type, content, metadata, created_at \
  1298→             FROM agent_events WHERE task_id = ?1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?2 OFFSET ?3",
  1299→            )
  1300→            .context("Failed to prepare get_agent_events")?;
  1301→        let rows = stmt.query_map(params![task_id, limit, offset], |row| {
  1302→            Ok((
  1303→                row.get::<_, i64>(0)?,
  1304→                row.get::<_, i64>(1)?,
  1305→                row.get::<_, String>(2)?,
  1306→                row.get::<_, String>(3)?,
  1307→                row.get::<_, Option<String>>(4)?,
  1308→                row.get::<_, String>(5)?,
  1309→            ))
  1310→        })?;
  1311→        let mut events = Vec::new();
  1312→        for row in rows {
  1313→            let (id, task_id, event_type_str, content, metadata_str, created_at) = row?;
  1314→            let metadata: Option<serde_json::Value> =
  1315→                match metadata_str {
  1316→                    Some(s) => Some(serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| {
  1317→                        anyhow::anyhow!("corrupt event metadata JSON '{}': {}", s, e)
  1318→                    })?),
  1319→                    None => None,
  1320→                };
  1321→            events.push(AgentEvent {
  1322→                id,
  1323→                task_id,
  1324→                event_type: event_type_str.parse().map_err(|_| {
  1325→                    anyhow::anyhow!("invalid event_type in database: '{}'", event_type_str)
  1326→                })?,
  1327→                content,
  1328→                metadata,
  1329→                created_at,
  1330→            });
  1331→        }
  1332→        Ok(events)
  1333→    }

conn.query(&sql, [iws.issue.id]).await?;\n            if let Some(row) = rows.next().await? {\n                iws.active_run = Some(row_to_pipeline_run(&row)?);\n            }\n        }\n\n        columns.push(ColumnView {\n            name: col.clone(),\n            issues: col_issues,\n        });\n    }\n\n    Ok(BoardView { project, columns })\n}\n\npub async fn get_issue_detail(conn: &Connection, id: i64) -> Result<Option<IssueDetail>> {\n    let issue = match get_issue(conn, id).await? {\n        Some(i) => i,\n        None => return Ok(None),\n    };\n\n    let sql = format!(\n        \"SELECT {} FROM pipeline_runs WHERE issue_id = ?1 ORDER BY id\",\n        RUN_COLS\n    );\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(&sql, [id])\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to query pipeline runs\")?;\n    let mut runs = Vec::new();\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next().await? {\n        let run = row_to_pipeline_run(&row)?;\n        let phases = super::pipeline::get_pipeline_phases(conn, run.id).await?;\n        runs.push(PipelineRunDetail { run, phases });\n    }\n\n    Ok(Some(IssueDetail { issue, runs }))\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::factory::db::DbHandle;\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_create_issue() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        let project = super::super::projects::create_project(&conn, \"test-proj\", \"/tmp/test-proj\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n\n        let issue = create_issue(\n            &conn,\n            project.id,\n            \"Fix bug #42\",\n            \"The login page crashes\",\n            &IssueColumn::Backlog,\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n        assert!(issue.id > 0);\n        assert_eq!(issue.project_id, project.id);\n        assert_eq!(issue.title, \"Fix bug #42\");\n        assert_eq!(issue.description, \"The login page crashes\");\n        assert_eq!(issue.column, IssueColumn::Backlog);\n        assert_eq!(issue.position, 0);\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_list_issues() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        let project = super::super::projects::create_project(&conn, \"test-proj\", \"/tmp/test-proj\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n\n        create_issue(&conn, project.id, \"Issue A\", \"\", &IssueColumn::Backlog)\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        create_issue(&conn, project.id, \"Issue B\", \"\", &IssueColumn::Backlog)\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n\n        let issues = list_issues(&conn, project.id).await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(issues.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(issues[0].title, \"Issue A\");\n        assert_eq!(issues[1].title, \"Issue B\");\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_move_issue() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        let project = super::super::projects::create_project(&conn, \"test\", \"/tmp/test\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let issue = create_issue(&conn, project.id, \"Move me\", \"\", &IssueColumn::Backlog)\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n\n        let moved = move_issue(&conn, issue.id, &IssueColumn::InProgress, 0)\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(moved.column, IssueColumn::InProgress);\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_soft_delete_issue() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        let project = super::super::projects::create_project(&conn, \"test\", \"/tmp/test\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let issue = create_issue(&conn, project.id, \"Delete me\", \"\", &IssueColumn::Backlog)\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n\n        let deleted = delete_issue(&conn, issue.id).await.unwrap();\n        assert!(deleted);\n\n        let fetched = get_issue(&conn, issue.id).await.unwrap();\n        assert!(fetched.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_update_issue() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        let project = super::super::projects::create_project(&conn, \"test\", \"/tmp/test\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let issue = create_issue(&conn, project.id, \"Old title\", \"\", &IssueColumn::Backlog)\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n\n        let updated = update_issue(&conn, issue.id, Some(\"New title\"), None, None, None)\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(updated.title, \"New title\");\n    }\n}\n"

  "content": "use std::str::FromStr;\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse libsql::{Connection, Row};\n\nuse crate::factory::models::*;\n\nfn row_to_pipeline_run(row: &Row) -> Result<PipelineRun> {\n    let status_str: String = row.get(2)?;\n    let status = PipelineStatus::from_str(&status_str)\n        .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))\n        .context(\"Failed to parse pipeline run status\")?;\n    let has_team: Option<i64> = row.get(11)?;\n    Ok(PipelineRun {\n        id: row.get(0)?,\n        issue_id: row.get(1)?,\n        status,\n        phase_count: row.get(3)?,\n        current_phase: row.get(4)?,\n        iteration: row.get(5)?,\n        summary: row.get(6)?,\n        error: row.get(7)?,\n        branch_name: row.get(8)?,\n        pr_url: row.get(9)?,\n        team_id: row.get(10)?,\n        has_team: has_team.map(|v| v != 0).unwrap_or(false),\n        started_at: row.get(12)?,\n        completed_at: row.get(13)?,\n    })\n}\n\nconst RUN_COLS: &str = \"id, issue_id, status, phase_count, current_phase, iteration, summary, error, branch_name, pr_url, team_id, has_team, started_at, completed_at\";\n\npub async fn create_pipeline_run(conn: &Connection, issue_id: i64) -> Result<PipelineRun> {\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO pipeline_runs (issue_id) VALUES (?1)\",\n        [issue_id],\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to insert pipeline run\")?;\n    let id = conn.last_insert_rowid();\n    get_pipeline_run(conn, id)\n        .await?\n        .context(\"Pipeline run not found after insert\")\n}\n\npub async fn update_pipeline_run(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    id: i64,\n    status: &PipelineStatus,\n    summary: Option<&str>,\n    error: Option<&str>,\n) -> Result<PipelineRun> {\n    if status.is_terminal() {\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE pipeline_runs SET status = ?1, summary = ?2, error = ?3, completed_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?4\",\n            libsql::params![status.as_str(), summary, error, id],\n        )\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to update pipeline run\")?;\n    } else {\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE pipeline_runs SET status = ?1, summary = ?2, error = ?3 WHERE id = ?4\",\n            libsql::params![status.as_str(), summary, error, id],\n        )\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to update pipeline run\")?;\n    }\n\n    get_pipeline_run(conn, id)\n        .await?\n        .context(\"Pipeline run not found after update\")\n}\n\npub async fn get_pipeline_run(conn: &Connection, id: i64) -> Result<Option<PipelineRun>> {\n    let sql = format!(\"SELECT {} FROM pipeline_runs WHERE id = ?1\", RUN_COLS);\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(&sql, [id])\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to query pipeline run\")?;\n    match rows.next().await? {\n        Some(row) => Ok(Some(row_to_pipeline_run(&row)?)),\n        None => Ok(None),\n    }\n}\n\npub async fn cancel_pipeline_run(conn: &Connection, id: i64) -> Result<PipelineRun> {\n    update_pipeline_run(conn, id, &PipelineStatus::Cancelled, None, None).await\n}\n\npub async fn recover_orphaned_runs(conn: &Connection) -> Result<usize> {\n    let count = conn.execute(\n        \"UPDATE pipeline_runs SET status = 'failed', error = 'Server restarted — pipeline process was lost', completed_at = datetime('now') WHERE status IN ('running', 'queued')\",\n        (),\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to recover orphaned pipeline runs\")?;\n\n    if count > 0 {\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE issues SET column_name = 'ready' WHERE column_name = 'in_progress' AND id IN (SELECT issue_id FROM pipeline_runs WHERE error = 'Server restarted — pipeline process was lost')\",\n            (),\n        )\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to move orphaned issues back to ready\")?;\n    }\n\n    Ok(count as usize)\n}\n\npub async fn update_pipeline_progress(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    id: i64,\n    phase_count: Option<i32>,\n    current_phase: Option<i32>,\n    iteration: Option<i32>,\n) -> Result<PipelineRun> {\n    conn.execute(\n        \"UPDATE pipeline_runs SET phase_count = ?1, current_phase = ?2, iteration = ?3 WHERE id = ?4\",\n        libsql::params![phase_count, current_phase, iteration, id],\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to update pipeline progress\")?;\n    get_pipeline_run(conn, id)\n        .await?\n        .context(\"Pipeline run not found after progress update\")\n}\n\npub async fn update_pipeline_branch(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    id: i64,\n    branch_name: &str,\n) -> Result<PipelineRun> {\n    conn.execute(\n        \"UPDATE pipeline_runs SET branch_name = ?1 WHERE id = ?2\",\n        (branch_name, id),\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to update pipeline branch\")?;\n    get_pipeline_run(conn, id)\n        .await?\n        .context(\"Pipeline run not found after branch update\")\n}\n\npub async fn update_pipeline_pr_url(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    id: i64,\n    pr_url: &str,\n) -> Result<PipelineRun> {\n    conn.execute(\n        \"UPDATE pipeline_runs SET pr_url = ?1 WHERE id = ?2\",\n        (pr_url, id),\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to update pipeline PR URL\")?;\n    get_pipeline_run(conn, id)\n        .await?\n        .context(\"Pipeline run not found after PR URL update\")\n}\n\npub async fn upsert_pipeline_phase(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    run_id: i64,\n    phase_number: &str,\n    phase_name: &str,\n    status: &str,\n    iteration: Option<i32>,\n    budget: Option<i32>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO pipeline_phases (run_id, phase_number, phase_name, status, iteration, budget, started_at)\n         VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, datetime('now'))\n         ON CONFLICT(run_id, phase_number) DO UPDATE SET\n            status = ?4,\n            iteration = COALESCE(?5, pipeline_phases.iteration),\n            budget = COALESCE(?6, pipeline_phases.budget),\n            completed_at = CASE WHEN ?4 IN ('completed', 'failed') THEN datetime('now') ELSE pipeline_phases.completed_at END,\n            error = NULL\",\n        libsql::params![run_id, phase_number, phase_name, status, iteration, budget],\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to upsert pipeline phase\")?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\npub async fn upsert_pipeline_phase_typed(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    run_id: i64,\n    phase_number: &str,\n    phase_name: &str,\n    status: &PhaseStatus,\n    iteration: Option<i32>,\n    budget: Option<i32>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    upsert_pipeline_phase(\n        conn,\n        run_id,\n        phase_number,\n        phase_name,\n        &status.to_string(),\n        iteration,\n        budget,\n    )\n    .await\n}\n\npub async fn get_pipeline_phases(conn: &Connection, run_id: i64) -> Result<Vec<PipelinePhase>> {\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(\n            \"SELECT id, run_id, phase_number, phase_name, status, iteration, budget, started_at, completed_at, error\n             FROM pipeline_phases WHERE run_id = ?1 ORDER BY phase_number ASC\",\n            [run_id],\n        )\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to query pipeline phases\")?;\n\n    let mut phases = Vec::new();\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next().await? {\n        let status_str: String = row.get(4)?;\n        let status = status_str.parse::<PhaseStatus>().unwrap_or(PhaseStatus::Pending);\n        phases.push(PipelinePhase {\n            id: row.get(0)?,\n            run_id: row.get(1)?,\n            phase_number: row.get(2)?,\n            phase_name: row.get(3)?,\n            status,\n            iteration: row.get(5)?,\n            budget: row.get(6)?,\n            started_at: row.get(7)?,\n            completed_at: row.get(8)?,\n            error: row.get(9)?,\n        });\n    }\n    Ok(phases)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::factory::db::DbHandle;\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_create_pipeline_run() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        let project = super::super::projects::create_project(&conn, \"test\", \"/tmp/test\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let issue = super::super::issues::create_issue(\n            &conn,\n            project.id,\n            \"Test issue\",\n            \"\",\n            &IssueColumn::Backlog,\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n\n        let run = create_pipeline_run(&conn, issue.id).await.unwrap();\n        assert!(run.id > 0);\n        assert_eq!(run.issue_id, issue.id);\n        assert_eq!(run.status, PipelineStatus::Queued);\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_update_pipeline_run() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        let project = super::super::projects::create_project(&conn, \"test\", \"/tmp/test\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let issue = super::super::issues::create_issue(\n            &conn,\n            project.id,\n            \"Test\",\n            \"\",\n            &IssueColumn::Backlog,\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n        let run = create_pipeline_run(&conn, issue.id).await.unwrap();\n\n        let updated =\n            update_pipeline_run(&conn, run.id, &PipelineStatus::Running, None, None)\n                .await\n                .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(updated.status, PipelineStatus::Running);\n\n        let completed = update_pipeline_run(\n            &conn,\n            run.id,\n            &PipelineStatus::Completed,\n            Some(\"All done\"),\n            None,\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(completed.status, PipelineStatus::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(completed.summary, Some(\"All done\".to_string()));\n        assert!(completed.completed_at.is_some());\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_upsert_pipeline_phase() {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        let project = super::super::projects::create_project(&conn, \"test\", \"/tmp/test\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let issue = super::super::issues::create_issue(\n            &conn,\n            project.id,\n            \"Test\",\n            \"\",\n            &IssueColumn::Backlog,\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n        let run = create_pipeline_run(&conn, issue.id).await.unwrap();\n\n        upsert_pipeline_phase(&conn, run.id, \"1\", \"Phase One\", \"running\", Some(1), Some(5))\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n\n        let phases = get_pipeline_phases(&conn, run.id).await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(phases.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(phases[0].phase_name, \"Phase One\");\n        assert_eq!(phases[0].status, PhaseStatus::Running);\n    }\n}\n"

row.get(14)?,\n    })\n}\n\npub async fn get_agent_tasks(conn: &Connection, team_id: i64) -> Result<Vec<AgentTask>> {\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(\n            \"SELECT id, team_id, name, description, agent_role, wave, depends_on, status, \\\n             isolation_type, worktree_path, container_id, branch_name, started_at, completed_at, error \\\n             FROM agent_tasks WHERE team_id = ?1 ORDER BY wave, id\",\n            [team_id],\n        )\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to query agent tasks\")?;\n    let mut tasks = Vec::new();\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next().await? {\n        tasks.push(row_to_agent_task(&row)?);\n    }\n    Ok(tasks)\n}\n\npub async fn get_agent_task(conn: &Connection, task_id: i64) -> Result<AgentTask> {\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(\n            \"SELECT id, team_id, name, description, agent_role, wave, depends_on, status, \\\n             isolation_type, worktree_path, container_id, branch_name, started_at, completed_at, error \\\n             FROM agent_tasks WHERE id = ?1\",\n            [task_id],\n        )\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to query agent task\")?;\n    let row = rows.next().await?.context(\"Agent task not found\")?;\n    row_to_agent_task(&row)\n}\n\npub async fn get_agent_team_detail(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    run_id: i64,\n) -> Result<Option<AgentTeamDetail>> {\n    let team = match get_agent_team_by_run(conn, run_id).await? {\n        Some(t) => t,\n        None => return Ok(None),\n    };\n    let tasks = get_agent_tasks(conn, team.id).await?;\n    Ok(Some(AgentTeamDetail { team, tasks }))\n}\n\npub async fn create_agent_event(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    task_id: i64,\n    event_type: &str,\n    content: &str,\n    metadata: Option<&serde_json::Value>,\n) -> Result<AgentEvent> {\n    let metadata_str = match metadata {\n        Some(m) => Some(serde_json::to_string(m).context(\"Failed to serialize event metadata\")?),\n        None => None,\n    };\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO agent_events (task_id, event_type, content, metadata) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)\",\n        libsql::params![task_id, event_type, content, metadata_str],\n    )\n    .await\n    .context(\"Failed to insert agent event\")?;\n    let id = conn.last_insert_rowid();\n    Ok(AgentEvent {\n        id,\n        task_id,\n        event_type: event_type\n            .parse()\n            .map_err(|_| anyhow::anyhow!(\"invalid event_type: '{}'\", event_type))?,\n        content: content.to_string(),\n        metadata: metadata.cloned(),\n        created_at: chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339(),\n    })\n}\n\npub async fn get_agent_events(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    task_id: i64,\n    limit: i64,\n    offset: i64,\n) -> Result<Vec<AgentEvent>> {\n    let mut rows = conn\n        .query(\n            \"SELECT id, task_id, event_type, content, metadata, created_at \\\n             FROM agent_events WHERE task_id = ?1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?2 OFFSET ?3\",\n            (task_id, limit, offset),\n        )\n        .await\n        .context(\"Failed to query agent events\")?;\n    let mut events = Vec::new();\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next().await? {\n        let event_type_str: String = row.get(2)?;\n        let metadata_str: Option<String> = row.get(4)?;\n        let metadata: Option<serde_json::Value> = match metadata_str {\n            Some(s) => Some(serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| {\n                anyhow::anyhow!(\"corrupt event metadata JSON '{}': {}\", s, e)\n            })?),\n            None => None,\n        };\n        events.push(AgentEvent {\n            id: row.get(0)?,\n            task_id: row.get(1)?,\n            event_type: event_type_str.parse().map_err(|_| {\n                anyhow::anyhow!(\"invalid event_type in database: '{}'\", event_type_str)\n            })?,\n            content: row.get(3)?,\n            metadata,\n            created_at: row.get(5)?,\n        });\n    }\n    Ok(events)\n}\n\n// Aliases for backward compatibility with callers\npub async fn get_agent_team_for_run(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    run_id: i64,\n) -> Result<Option<AgentTeamDetail>> {\n    get_agent_team_detail(conn, run_id).await\n}\n\npub async fn get_agent_events_for_task(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    task_id: i64,\n    limit: i64,\n) -> Result<Vec<AgentEvent>> {\n    get_agent_events(conn, task_id, limit, 0).await\n}\n\npub async fn insert_agent_event(\n    conn: &Connection,\n    task_id: i64,\n    event_type: &str,\n    content: &str,\n    metadata: Option<&serde_json::Value>,\n) -> Result<AgentEvent> {\n    create_agent_event(conn, task_id, event_type, content, metadata).await\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::factory::db::DbHandle;\n\n    async fn setup() -> (DbHandle, libsql::Connection, PipelineRun) {\n        let db = DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap();\n        let conn = db.conn().unwrap();\n        let project = super::super::projects::create_project(&conn, \"test\", \"/tmp/test\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let issue = super::super::issues::create_issue(\n            &conn,\n            project.id,\n            \"Test\",\n            \"\",\n            &IssueColumn::Backlog,\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n        let run = super::super::pipeline::create_pipeline_run(&conn, issue.id)\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        (db, conn, run)\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_create_agent_team() {\n        let (_db, conn, run) = setup().await;\n        let team = create_agent_team(\n            &conn,\n            run.id,\n            &ExecutionStrategy::WavePipeline,\n            &IsolationStrategy::Worktree,\n            \"Test plan\",\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n        assert!(team.id > 0);\n        assert_eq!(team.run_id, run.id);\n        assert_eq!(team.strategy, ExecutionStrategy::WavePipeline);\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_create_and_get_agent_task() {\n        let (_db, conn, run) = setup().await;\n        let team = create_agent_team(\n            &conn,\n            run.id,\n            &ExecutionStrategy::Sequential,\n            &IsolationStrategy::Shared,\n            \"\",\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n\n        let task = create_agent_task(\n            &conn,\n            team.id,\n            \"Implement feature\",\n            \"Write the code\",\n            &AgentRole::Coder,\n            0,\n            &[],\n            &IsolationStrategy::Shared,\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n        assert!(task.id > 0);\n        assert_eq!(task.status, AgentTaskStatus::Pending);\n\n        let fetched = get_agent_task(&conn, task.id).await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(fetched.name, \"Implement feature\");\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_update_agent_task_status() {\n        let (_db, conn, run) = setup().await;\n        let team = create_agent_team(\n            &conn,\n            run.id,\n            &ExecutionStrategy::Sequential,\n            &IsolationStrategy::Shared,\n            \"\",\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n        let task = create_agent_task(\n            &conn,\n            team.id,\n            \"Task\",\n            \"\",\n            &AgentRole::Coder,\n            0,\n            &[],\n            &IsolationStrategy::Shared,\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n\n        update_agent_task_status(&conn, task.id, &AgentTaskStatus::Running, None)\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let fetched = get_agent_task(&conn, task.id).await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(fetched.status, AgentTaskStatus::Running);\n        assert!(fetched.started_at.is_some());\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_agent_events() {\n        let (_db, conn, run) = setup().await;\n        let team = create_agent_team(\n            &conn,\n            run.id,\n            &ExecutionStrategy::Sequential,\n            &IsolationStrategy::Shared,\n            \"\",\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n        let task = create_agent_task(\n            &conn,\n            team.id,\n            \"Task\",\n            \"\",\n            &AgentRole::Coder,\n            0,\n 10, 0).await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(events.len(), 2);\n        // Ordered by id DESC\n        assert_eq!(events[0].content, \"Editing file\");\n        assert_eq!(events[1].content, \"Hello world\");\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_agent_team_detail() {\n        let (_db, conn, run) = setup().await;\n        let team = create_agent_team(\n            &conn,\n      1→pub mod agents;
     2→pub mod issues;
     3→pub mod migrations;
     4→pub mod pipeline;
     5→pub mod projects;
     6→pub mod settings;
     7→
     8→use std::path::Path;
     9→use std::time::Duration;
    10→
    11→use anyhow::{Context, Result};
    12→use libsql::{Builder, Connection, Database};
    13→
    14→/// Async database handle wrapping a libsql Database.
    15→///
    16→/// Supports two modes:
    17→/// - **Turso embedded replica** when TURSO_DATABASE_URL and TURSO_AUTH_TOKEN are set
    18→/// - **Local SQLite** otherwise (dev/offline fallback)
    19→#[derive(Clone)]
    20→pub struct DbHandle {
    21→    db: Database,
    22→}
    23→
    24→impl DbHandle {
    25→    /// Open a local-only SQLite database at the given path.
    26→    pub async fn new_local(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
    27→        let path_str = path.to_string_lossy().to_string();
    28→        let db = Builder::new_local(&path_str)
    29→            .build()
    30→            .await
    31→            .context("Failed to open local SQLite database")?;
    32→        let handle = Self { db };
    33→        handle.init().await?;
    34→        Ok(handle)
    35→    }
    36→
    37→    /// Open a Turso embedded replica with local file cache.
    38→    pub async fn new_remote_replica(
    39→        local_path: &Path,
    40→        url: &str,
    41→        token: &str,
    42→    ) -> Result<Self> {
    43→        let path_str = local_path.to_string_lossy().to_string();
    44→        let db = Builder::new_remote_replica(
    45→            &path_str,
    46→            url.to_string(),
    47→            token.to_string(),
    48→        )
    49→        .sync_interval(Duration::from_secs(60))
    50→        .read_your_writes(true)
    51→        .build()
    52→        .await
    53→        .context("Failed to open Turso embedded replica")?;
    54→        let handle = Self { db };
    55→        handle.init().await?;
    56→        Ok(handle)
    57→    }
    58→
    59→    /// Open an in-memory database (for testing).
    60→    pub async fn new_in_memory() -> Result<Self> {
    61→        let db = Builder::new_local(":memory:")
    62→            .build()
    63→            .await
    64→            .context("Failed to open in-memory database")?;
    65→        let handle = Self { db };
    66→        handle.init().await?;
    67→        Ok(handle)
    68→    }
    69→
    70→    /// Get a new connection from the database.
    71→    pub fn conn(&self) -> Result<Connection> {
    72→        self.db
    73→            .connect()
    74→            .context("Failed to get database connection")
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    /// Sync embedded replica with remote (no-op for local-only).
    78→    pub async fn sync(&self) -> Result<()> {
    79→        // sync() returns a result with replication info; we just need it to succeed
    80→        let _ = self.db.sync().await;
    81→        Ok(())
    82→    }
    83→
    84→    /// Initialize pragmas and run migrations.
    85→    async fn init(&self) -> Result<()> {
    86→        let conn = self.conn()?;
    87→        init_pragmas(&conn).await?;
    88→        migrations::run_migrations(&conn).await?;
    89→        Ok(())
    90→    }
    91→}
    92→
    93→/// Set WAL mode, busy timeout, and enable foreign keys.
    94→async fn init_pragmas(conn: &Connection) -> Result<()> {
    95→    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL", ())
    96→        .await
    97→        .context("Failed to set WAL mode")?;
    98→    conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000", ())
    99→        .await
   100→        .context("Failed to set busy_timeout")?;
   101→    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON", ())
   102→        .await
   103→        .context("Failed to enable foreign keys")?;
   104→    Ok(())
   105→}
   106→
   107→/// Run integrity check on startup. Logs warning but does not fail.
   108→pub async fn health_check(conn: &Connection) {
   109→    match conn.query("PRAGMA integrity_check", ()).await {
   110→        Ok(mut rows) => {
   111→            if let Ok(Some(row)) = rows.next().await {
   112→                if let Ok(result) = row.get::<String>(0) {
   113→                    if result != "ok" {
   114→                        tracing::warn!("Database integrity check failed: {result}");
   115→                    }
   116→                }
   117→            }
   118→        }
   119→        Err(e) => {
   120→            tracing::warn!("Failed to run integrity check: {e}");
   121→        }
   122→    }
   123→}
   124→

All changes are complete. Here is a summary of what was done in `/Users/jdsingh/Projects/AI/forge/src/factory/api.rs`:

**Imports:**
- Removed the `#[cfg(test)] use super::db::FactoryDb;` import (line 17-18). `FactoryDb` is no longer referenced anywhere.
- Kept `use super::db::DbHandle;`.

**Handler migrations (18 `db.call()` patterns replaced):**

1. `do_sync_github_issues` -- 3 calls:
   - `db.get_project(project_id)` extracted from closure, with `.ok_or_else()` for the not-found case
   - `db.update_project_github_repo(project_id, owner_repo)` direct async call
   - Loop of `db.create_issue_from_github(...)` -- replaced batch closure with a `for` loop calling each one individually with `.await`

2. `list_projects` -- `db.list_projects().await`
3. `create_project` -- `db.create_project(&name, &path).await`
4. `clone_project` -- multi-step closure replaced with sequential calls: `db.list_projects()`, `db.create_project()`, `db.update_project_github_repo()`
5. `get_project` -- `db.get_project(id).await`
6. `delete_project` -- `db.delete_project(id).await`
7. `get_board` -- `db.get_board(id).await`
8. `create_issue` -- `db.create_issue(...).await`
9. `get_issue` -- `db.get_issue_detail(id).await`
10. `update_issue` -- `db.update_issue(...).await`
11. `move_issue` -- split into two sequential calls: `db.get_issue(id).await` then `db.move_issue(id, &column, position).await`
12. `delete_issue` -- `db.delete_issue(id).await`
13. `trigger_pipeline` -- split into `db.get_issue(issue_id).await` then `db.create_pipeline_run(issue_id).await`; re-fetch uses `db.get_pipeline_run(run_id).await`
14. `get_pipeline_run` -- `db.get_pipeline_run(id).await`
15. `get_run_team` -- `db.get_agent_team_for_run(run_id).await`
16. `get_run_phases` -- split into `db.get_pipeline_run(run_id).await` then `db.get_pipeline_phases(run_id).await`
17. `get_task_events` -- `db.get_agent_events_for_task(task_id, limit).await`
18. `serve_screenshot` -- `db.list_projects().await` with `.first().map(...).ok_or_else(...)`
19. `github_connect_token` -- `db.set_setting("github_token", &token).await`
20. `github_disconnect` -- `db.delete_setting("github_token").await`

**Tests:**
- `test_app()` converted from `fn` to `async fn`, using `DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap()` instead of `FactoryDb::new_in_memory()` + `DbHandle::new(db)`
- All 15 call sites of `test_app()` updated to `test_app().await`
- `test_create_issue_broadcasts_ws` -- inline state creation updated to use `DbHandle::new_in_memory().await`
- `test_github_token_persisted_in_settings` -- converted from sync `FactoryDb` calls to async `DbHandle` calls with `.await`

Here is a summary of all changes made to `/Users/jdsingh/Projects/AI/forge/src/factory/pipeline.rs`:

**Import cleanup:**
- Removed `FactoryDb` from the test import: `use super::super::db::{DbHandle, FactoryDb}` became `use super::super::db::DbHandle`

**Production code -- `cancel()` method:**
- `db.call(move |db| db.cancel_pipeline_run(run_id)).await?` became `db.cancel_pipeline_run(run_id).await?`
- `db.call(move |db| { Ok(db.get_issue(issue_id)?.is_some_and(...)) }).await?` became `db.get_issue(issue_id).await?.is_some_and(...)`
- `db.call(move |db| db.move_issue(issue_id, &IssueColumn::Ready, 0)).await` became `db.move_issue(issue_id, &IssueColumn::Ready, 0).await`

**Production code -- `execute_agent_team()` method:**
- Multi-statement `db.call` closure creating team + tasks was unwrapped into sequential `db.create_agent_team(...).await?` and a loop of `db.create_agent_task(...).await?` calls

**Production code -- `start_run()` method:**
- Project lookup `db.call(move |db| match db.get_project(...))` became `match db.get_project(project_id).await`
- Combined `update_pipeline_run` + `move_issue` closure became two sequential async calls
- `db.call(move |db| db.update_pipeline_branch(...))` became `db.update_pipeline_branch(...).await`
- Cancellation check `db.call(move |db| { Ok(db.get_pipeline_run(...)) })` became `db.get_pipeline_run(run_id).await.map(...)`
- PR URL update `db.call(move |db| { db.update_pipeline_pr_url(...) })` became `db.update_pipeline_pr_url(...).await`
- Completion closure (update_pipeline_run + move_issue) became two sequential async calls
- Failure closure `db.call({ ... move |db| { db.update_pipeline_run(...) } })` became `db.update_pipeline_run(...).await`

**Production code -- `execute_pipeline_streaming()` and Docker pipeline:**
- Two `db.call(move |db| { db.update_pipeline_progress(...) })` calls became `db.update_pipeline_progress(...).await`

**Production code -- `handle_phase_event()`:**
- Three `db.call(move |db| { db.upsert_pipeline_phase(...) })` calls became `db.upsert_pipeline_phase(...).await` (also removed unnecessary `.clone()` / owned variable intermediaries)

**Test code:**
- All 6 `FactoryDb::new_in_memory().unwrap()` + `DbHandle::new(db)` patterns became `DbHandle::new_in_memory().await.unwrap()`
- All synchronous `db.create_project(...)`, `db.create_issue(...)`, `db.create_pipeline_run(...)`, etc. became `.await` calls
- `db.lock_sync().unwrap()` + `db_guard.get_pipeline_run(...)` became `db.get_pipeline_run(...).await`
- `setup_test_db()` became `async fn setup_test_db()` with all internal calls awaited
- All 4 call sites updated to `setup_test_db().await`

The file compiles cleanly with no pipeline.rs-specific errors (remaining errors are in `server.rs` and `agent_executor.rs` which are separate migration tasks).
